Haṁsadūta Swami

## Harikeśa Swami

**Śrīla Prabhupāda-saṁhitā**

(First 7 verses only)  
\[Sung in the *Brahma-saṁhitā* chant meter\]

**(1)** This world of darkness was devoid of the hope for freedom,  
The mass of people overwhelmed by the dense illusion  
Until the moment when you landed in New York harbor  
Then hope was dawning like the sun, Prabhupada namo namas te.

**(2)** The external energy of Kṛṣṇa in the form of matter  
Conceals the living entity from his real nature;  
He will burn in the fire of maya totally bewildered  
Until doused in your cloud of mercy, Prabhupada namo namas te.

**(3)** You brought us to reality asking us to taste it  
While imploring us to see what’s fantasy and reject it;  
You exposed our desires to enjoy and control like Kṛṣṇa  
And we fell at your lotus feet in thanks, Prabhupada namo namas te.

**(4)** We are eternal servants of the Lord—-not all the master;  
You engaged us in serving Him to make us learn it faster;  
Everything we needed to know about Kṛṣṇa you told us;  
Through you we served the Lord, Prabhupada namo namas te.

**(5)** Our lives become complete if we wish to fulfill your mission;  
We must print and distribute all your books without reservation  
In every language of the world every book completed  
Till everyone has a full set, Prabhupada namo namas te.

**(6)** Teaching the message of the Lord everywhere under all conditions  
And bringing all the people within your spiritual dominion,  
Engaging them in serving Kṛṣṇa in the varṇāśrama system  
We will finally attain your mercy, Prabhupada namo namas te.

**(7)** Prabhupada you have left us with the order to battle maya  
And by your mercy we are burning with intense desire  
To transform this dark world burdened with cheating rascal demons  
To a place where all people chant, Prabhupada namo namas te.

*Your eternal servant,*  
Harikeśa Swami

## Hṛdayānanda dāsaGoswami

O Prabhupada,  
By your absence, illusion is threatening the world  
Kṛṣṇa is everywhere, within and without,  
But who can understand or please Kṛṣṇa without your mercy?  
Who can receive your mercy if he does not obey you, or even  
Hear you?

O Prabhupada,  
You created a brilliant sun, your ISKCON;  
You brought Kṛṣṇa to America, Europe, South America, Asia,  
Australia and Africa, and even back to India, Kṛṣṇa’s own land;  
Just as Pṛthu Maharaja brought Viṣṇu to his sacrificial arena, by his devotion  
By your devotion, there was light and victory everywhere.

O Prabhupada,  
Surely your desire is that we struggle to expand your holy mission;  
“Book distribution is the best preaching,” you insistently told us;  
“Your main duty is to print and distribute books,” you told me personally;  
In the absence of book distribution, even big sannyāsīs go astray.

O Prabhupada,  
You requested me to write and preach and chant about Kṛṣṇa;  
Thus you opened the doorway to Kṛṣṇaloka by showing the path of  
śravaṇaṁ kīrtanaṁ viṣṇoh,  
But your tongue is a frog’s tongue that croaks nonsense, calling Death near;  
O Prabhupada, let us be as you are and not waste precious time.

O Prabhupada,  
The kingdom of illusion is enjoying the opportunity of your absence;  
Maya could not touch you, O Jagad-guru, as you circled the globe like  
An invulnerable angry elephant;  
Maya retreated before your fierce preaching, and there was victory  
Everywhere;  
Now in your absence, if we neglect your order for a moment, we may  
Be plundered and deviated, and illusion will return.

O Prabhupada,  
We cannot bear the suffering of your separation;  
Your mercy is causeless upon the most fallen; so please let your cause-  
Less mercy immediately capture our hearts;  
We are crying under the stinging reaction of our offenses;  
O Prabhupada, you are the most merciful, and the time is right to  
Show your mercy.

O Prabhupada,  
The suffering living entities desperately require your mercy;  
All hope is gone in the world without you, and without your causeless  
Mercy, the horizon is an ominous dark color;  
You can immediately save the world by invoking the mercy of the  
Almighty Supreme.  
O Prabhupada, glance upon us, we are helplessly floundering in your  
Absence.

O Prabhupada,  
You are the only object of my faith and hope.  
Kṛṣṇa is impossible to achieve by any other means.  
You are my life and my solution;  
Offering myself as your insignificant servant at your lotus feet, I beg  
You are a favorable response.

*Your eternal servant,*  
Hṛdayānanda dāsa Goswami

## Jagadīśa dāsa

Lord Nityānanda’s mercy  
Has manifest through you  
To capture all the fallen  
And bring them “back to home.”

The secret of devotion  
To Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet  
That’s unalloyed and constant  
You’ve given us complete.

Your words have depth of meaning  
For those who scrutinize  
That hardly can be fathomed  
By those who are most wise.

You’ve opened up the passage,  
Vṛndāvana can be reached  
By following your footsteps  
With unrelenting faith.

Not only have you opened  
The passage to Goloka,  
You’ve organized a movement  
That has gigantic scope.

A break in Kali-yuga  
Is manifesting now.  
The sun of Lord Caitanya  
Is shining through the clouds.

The beneficent program  
Of Gaura and Nitāi  
Is spreading very quickly  
Because of your desire.

Their influence is spreading.  
No iron wall can stop.  
The world is being flooded,  
No corner will be safe.

The darkness is retreating .  
The light is flooding here.  
Both sun and moon are shining  
Because you are so dear.

Jagadīśa dāsa.

## Jagajīvana Goswami

This struggling life  
A world of strife  
Life’s plans all gone askew.

Then by chance  
From maya’s glance  
We leave and come to you.

And in that dawn  
Of your sweet song  
I hope will come to pass.

That some day soon  
I get the boon  
Of serving you at last.

O Prabhupada  
My darkened heart  
Cries out to be with you.

My life, my soul  
My everything  
Condemned. What can I do?

But if I do  
As you have done  
Remembering what you’ve told

I’ll be with you  
And serve you too  
And heal this sickened soul.

Jagajīvana Goswami

## Jayapatāka Swami

*My dear spiritual master Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

On this most auspicious day of Your Divine Grace’s VYĀSA PŪJĀ I beg to offer you my prostrate obeisances unlimited times. Just as personally Your Divine Grace would guide us, now we are guided by your instructions and causeless mercy. As we sincerely try to follow your instructions, we can feel and understand your divine presence and mercy. Your instructions are practically unlimited, and your order to expand the propaganda of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s movement unlimitedly is the greatest inspiration, an inconceivably serious responsibility.

Our Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa instructed His dear devotee Arjuna on the Kuruksetra battlefield to fight on Hid order without considering “winning or losing.” The Lord revealed His universal (*viśva-rūpa)* form and informed and visually displayed to Arjuna that even if Arjuna decided not to fight all the soldiers and kings present on the battlefield were to be killed-were already killed—by the plan of the Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Has Arjuna neglected to follow Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s order, the Lord would have seen His plan successful in another way.

Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu predicted, and it is His plan, that the *saṅkīrtana* movement shall be established in every town and village around the world. Your Divine Grace is the transparent and pure representative of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Your order is as good as the Lord’s order. If we surrender to simply carrying out your order, being instruments in the Lord’s hands, this will be our glory. Then the “ISKCON Branch of Lord Caitanya’s Transcendental Tree” shall be the instrument and gain the credit for fulfilling Śrī Caitanya’s prediction! We shall be satisfied knowing Your Divine Grace, along with our previous *ācāryas* will be pleased.

Śrīla Prabhupāda! May I take this opportunity to beat any unsurrendered thoughts and desires out of my mind with shoes and brooms a hundred times—-and fall at your lotus-foot dust. Bless me that I may keep your divine instructions as my only guide—-that your idea fill my heart, leaving me no room for *māyā’s* ideas to enter. When will I remain by your side always as Your Divine Grace has commanded? Keep me at your lotus feet always serving your desire.

All glories to Your Divine Grace! *Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your insignificant servant,*  
Jayapatāka Swami

## Jayatīrtha Gowami

A song of love offered on the most auspicious occasion, the appearance in this world of the great divine preceptor, His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupada.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, My lord! Śrīla Prabhupāda, My friend!

**(1)** Just when the world seemed darkest  
On the verge of a flaming tempest  
In each soul a growing unrest  
Each in fear of the coming test.  
All cause to curse. No cause to bless.  
*Śrīla Prabhupāda, our lord! Śrīla Prabhupāda, our friend!*

**(2)** A just Lord would surely condemn  
This sinner and the world around him.  
Our role of wrong, so long, so grim,  
Our piety, so weak, so dim,  
A hanging judge? A rope, a limb.  
*Śrīla Prabhupāda, our lord! Śrīla Prabhupāda, our friend!*

**(3)** No other then causeless mercy  
Could help us….utterly guilty…  
Could liberate, could set us free  
Could make us taste eternity,  
Such mercy?.....Nowhere in history.  
*Śrīla Prabhupāda, our lord! Śrīla Prabhupāda, our friend!*

**(4)** We had no reason to suspect,  
We could not rightfully expect,  
We’ve tried our hardest to reject,  
Your mercy so sublime, perfect,  
The world not yet aware… Not yet!  
*Śrīla Prabhupāda, our lord! Śrīla Prabhupāda, our friend!*

**(5)** In the future days of struggle,  
Through the turmoil and the trouble,  
As the world begins to bubble,  
Cause our faith and love to double,  
And to double and to double.  
*Śrīla Prabhupāda, our lord! Śrīla Prabhupāda, our friend!*

**(6)** The most precious gift has been sent  
From the Lord of love transcendent  
To this dark world….destruction bent,  
A ship of pure love has been lent  
Your feet…so pure and innocent.  
*Śrīla Prabhupāda, our lord! Śrīla Prabhupāda, our friend!*

**(7)** In the center of existence,  
Beyond the land of resistance  
Where Kṛṣṇa sports in resplendence  
Your Divine Grace in attendance  
A bond of love and remembrance.  
*Śrīla Prabhupāda, our lord! Śrīla Prabhupāda, our friend!*

**(8)** Will ever this great pretender  
At last agree to surrender  
And humbling himself, so enter  
The secret, divine heart center  
To serve your lotus feet…nectar.  
*Śrīla Prabhupāda, our lord! Śrīla Prabhupāda, our friend!*

**(9)** May the world still soundly sleeping,  
Pains of sin still sadly reaping,  
Through their laughing and their weeping  
Hear the promise that you’re keeping  
Through these verses offered kneeling.  
*Śrīla Prabhupāda, our lord! Śrīla Prabhupāda, our friend!*

*The useless soul,*  
Jayatīrtha dāsa Goswami

With thanks to my Godbrothers, Śrīman Mahākṣa dāsa adhikārī and Śrīman Havi dāsa adhikārī, for the inspiration for this song.

## Kīrtanānanda Swami

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept my most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. Again I find myself trying to glorify your lordship but feeling wholly inadequate to express my debt of gratitude to you. It has been fifteen years since you picked me our of the muck and mire of New York’s Lower East Side and transported me to the world of Kṛṣṇaloka by your divine instructions and divine message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. With what can I repay you?

I remember sitting in your back room in the apartment at 26 Second Avenue. It was only a few days after I had met Your Divine Grace, and feeling that this was it, I blurted out, “Swamiji, should I renounce everything now?” You looked at me, and laughed and said, “What do you have to renounce?” In one instant you cut through all our illusions and laid bare the truth of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. How can we repay you?

Almost half a century ago, you wrote a VYĀSA PŪJĀ poem for your beloved Gurudeva, and one *śloka* pleased him especially. It reads:

Absolute is sentient, thou hast proved  
Impersonal calamity thou hast moved  
This gives us a life anew and fresh  
Worship they feet, Your Divine Grace

However true this is about Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvati Prabhupada, this is even more true about you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We were so immersed in Māyāvāda impersonalism that we didn’t even know what you were talking about. We even used to joke amongst ourselves. “Oh, Swamiji must have had some conflict in India with these people called Māyāvādīs.” We didn’t see what relevance it had to us.

And then one day in *Bhagavat-gītā* class after a mighty refutation of Rādhākrishnan’s commentary on that *śloka* from the Ninth Chapter where Kṛṣṇa says, “Engage your mind always in thinking of Me, offer obeisances and worship Me, being completely absorbed in Me, surely you will come to Me,” you began to quote Dr. Rādhākrishnan’s commentary which begins, “It is not Kṛṣṇa that we have to surrender…”and that is as far as you got. You exploded with a righteous indignation at the rascal who dares to say it is not to Kṛṣṇa that we have to surrender. We could not understand; yet after you had finished your great explanation, we raised our hands and tried to give our own mentally-concocted explanations as to how Rādhākrishanan and you were saying the same thing. You listened so patiently but with your anger rising, until finally you said, “Are you through?” Unfortunately, I wasn’t simply praying at your lotus feet that we may be thorough with this disease of Māyāvāda impersonalism.

For, indeed, you have proved the absolute sentience of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa beyond any doubt. You have given us a new life of devotional service. You have so mercifully allowed us to build you a Palace and even to install you there as King. Who can doubt that you are the King? You are the king of the modes of material nature. You are the king of your senses. You are the visible manifestation of the king of the cowherd boys. You conquered the three worlds for Lord Caitanya by your wonderful faith in Kṛṣṇa and your spiritual master. You have conquered the hearts of all your devotees. Certainly you are not attracted by a Palace of Gold or even of gems. We know this means nothing to you…you are the most renounced. But we, your most fallen disciples, need your lordship to rule over us and accept our service eternally.

That’s your right, you have the mace. Please, my dear Lord, take up your mace. Command, chastise, do whatever you like. I only pray that somehow or other we can always remain

*Your insignificant subject,*  
Kīrtanānanda Swami

## Pañcadravidā Swami

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

While chanting with you on the roof of the small temple in Delhi, I noticed the many lawns below of the neighboring houses. “Śrīla Prabhupāda,” I said, “this reminds me very much of Southern California.” Displeased, you turned to me and said, “Wherever you are, it will always look like California.” Obviously jolted by your reply, I managed to say, “And wherever you are, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you will see Vaikuṇṭha.” Without even moving your head, you continued walking, and said, “Yes.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you once said of your spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvati Thākura, “What can I say about him? He was a Vaikuntha man.” That description applies equally to yourself, who to mundane vision, might have appeared as a scholarly Indian *svāmī,* or to others as a humble Vaiṣṇavas, but who in truth is the most exalted of the residents of the spiritual world, Goloka Vṛndāvana, but who out of your causeless mercy, descended to save, and, in turn, teach others to save, such fallen and foolish conditioned souls as myself. Were it not for your mercy, I would have an endless cycle of lives as germs, fungi, plants, cockroaches, worms, parasites, dogs, hogs, cats, etc., as my future. But somehow or other, being guided by your transcendental hands, I am clumsily stumbling, bungling down the right path, back home, back to Godhead

The clock is ticking, and the seconds are racing away, like grains of sand lost in the hourglass. I want to learn how to serve you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to feel the touch of your hand upon my head, but what can I do? I am so condemned, bought and sold to *māyā* , that it is my gnawing fear that “How shall I find you again, Śrīla Prabhupāda? Where can I go to meet you?” I am burning in this material world, consumed by the fires of lust, greed, and wrath, and I have disqualified myself to have refuge at your lotus feet. I am most unworthy to serve you. But somehow, despite all my doubts, I know that you will not forget me, and will call me to you again.

We are walking in the sands Raman Reti, down the path by the Yamuna, where Kṛṣṇa and Balarama play. Around me the breeze murmurs, but I cannot see you. Everywhere is your name. Your name is there on the road, Bhaktivedanta Swami Marg. The peacocks are calling your name repeatedly, “Śrīla Prabhupāda, Śrīla Prabhupāda.” Your name is on everyone’s lips, “*Jaya,* Śrīla Prabhupāda.” You are always in Vṛndāvana, with Kṛṣṇa. One whose eyes are anointed with the salve of desire tree in Vṛndāvana. When my eyes are smeared with the ointment of devotional service, then I will again see you.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, kindly cast your glance upon your surrendered servitors in Latin America, and bless us so that we may continue executing your will. Falling in the dust of your lotus feet, I beg to remain,

*Your eternal servant,*  
Pañcadraviḍa Swami

## Prabhupāda-kṛpa Goswami

**(1)** No one to help you for seventy years,  
You presses on and on without any fears.  
Your guru’s order was your inspiration,  
To spread Kṛṣṇa’s name throughout every nation.

**(2)** You were always convinced everything that you did  
Would always be a successful bid,  
For helping others to see life’s goal  
And free them from illusion’s toll.

**(3)** An angel of mercy, that is you,  
Full of good qualities, thro’and thro’.  
With never a thought of self liberation,  
Nitya-baddha’s relief your consideration.

**(4)** Contemporaries like Bose, Gandhi and others,  
Could never free men from māyā’s brothers,  
Ignorant and foolish, they could only confuse;  
Their material formulas were simply a ruse.

**(5)** But the world comprised of cheaters and cheated,  
Gave them a hearing and became defeated.  
But you didn’t give up, you never became angry,  
You constantly wrote, to all and to sundry.

**(6)** You showed social improvement and altruism  
Could never fee anyone form the material prison.  
Material scientists’ value you reduced to nil;  
You showed their intention was simply to kill.

**(7)** By preaching the dharma of the Bhāgavatam  
You revealed their politics to be simply a sham.  
And through not responding to his “unknown friend”  
“Mahatma” Gandhi soon met his bitter end.

**(8)** A “voice in the wilderness” to a government hand,  
Requesting to take all “Back to Godhead.”  
But none of them listened to your earnest petitions,  
They thought to ignore you, in their big positions.

**(9)** Severely afflicted by maya’s confusion,  
They thought it was you who was in illusion,  
To save the whole world was your stated scheme;  
To these faithless men, it was simply a dream.

**(10)** Uncooperative family never caused you to fall—  
When no one listened you preached to the wall.  
Their failure to help was all Kṛṣṇa’s plan,  
So that you could save the family of man.

**(11)** Godbrothers wanted also to restrict your mission  
Showed themselves lacking your mahatma’s vision.  
They wanted you to serve, but not to speak;  
‘cause in their own preaching they were very weak.

**(12)** As Vṛndāvana’s resident for many days,  
You were fully absorbed in thinking of ways  
To present Kṛṣṇa’s message in foreign lands  
And thus fulfill your guru’s plans.

**(13)** Struggling alone in abject poverty  
You saw the whole world as Kṛṣṇa’s property.  
But with your two hands and a fountain pen,  
You could promise all wealth, because Kṛṣṇa has ten.

**(14)** So you continued to write your spiritual newspaper,  
Struggling so hard, but our future savior.  
No one could see your exalted position,  
A pure devotee with a specific mission.

**(15)** For all those years, your identity covered,  
You rendered loving service to your beloved  
Spiritual master and Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi,  
While Vṛndāvana “bābājīs” would foolishly cry.

**(16)** “O Swamiji, please! Stop this endeavor,  
Once in Vṛndāvana then you should never  
Think of leaving this dhāma, this holy place.  
Forget this preaching and the human race!

**(17)** Think of yourself, you should now retire!”  
But you ignored them, thinking from maya.  
A Vaisnava is happy seeing the pleasure of others;  
For the “para-dukha-dukhī” preaching’s never a bother.

**(18)** Thus came your first book, an “Easy Journey,”  
And thro’ your writing came power of attorney,  
To represent Kṛṣṇa, the transcendent Lord,  
And through these books you’d freed Kali’s hoard.

**(19)** The sinful men who were as good as dead  
Could all be revived through “Back to Godhead.”  
An authorized version of Bhagavad-gītā  
Would make their lives unlimitedly sweeter.

**(20)** In a small room in Rādhā-Damodara’s temple,  
You worked hard to follow Śrīla Rūpa’s example.  
In time appeared Bhagavatam’s one; two and three.  
Then immediately after, your passage at sea.

**(21)** At the fag end of life, your mission began,  
The journey itself would have killed a lesser man.  
Your pure heart contained Kṛṣṇa, He was always near  
And by His divine grace, you reached Boston’s pier.

**(22)** Since then, of course, there is no mystery,  
You get top honors in the world’s history.  
You’ll go down in the books that tell of the great  
As the one who saved all, from a terrible fate.

**(23)** You’ve shown all how to live with complete success,  
The lowest of men you were able to impress  
That life has meaning , it’s not mere chance,  
We can realize this through song and a dance.

**(24)** Worshiping Kṛṣṇa brings happiness in life,  
Frees one completely from all forms of strife;  
And finishes up repeated birth and death,  
Chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa means “Back to Godhead!”

**(25)** Now you have left us your preaching mantle,  
We all now should follow your pure example.  
You’ve done all the hard work, we should never complain;  
By your glorious endeavor, we got mercy’s rain.

**(26)** Our duty now is to spread your glory,  
Tell all the people your wonderful story.  
Expose them all to Kṛṣṇa’s śakti  
Manifested by you in the form of bhakti.

**(27)** Now let your flag of victory unfurl  
To show that your love has conquered the world!

*All glories to you Śrīla Prabhupāda!*

Prabhupāda-kṛpa Goswami

## Rāmeśvara Swami

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept my humble obeisances eternally in the dust of your lotus feet. Recently I was staying with a small band of devotees in Japan, confronted with an entire nation that has lost all faith in God due to bitter memories of their devastating defeat in World War II and due to the insistence of modern scientists that the origin of life is ultimately formless. The entire world has become so contaminated by this conception that even religionists are doubting the personal conception of God, and, therefore, practically all human beings are covered by the darkest ignorance, without any understanding about the actual purpose of life. Your appearance in the world during these dark times is more illuminating than thousands of moons. The Supreme Personality of Godhead sent you, like His blazing Sudarśana *cakra,* to deliver all conditioned souls from their greatest enemy—ignorance. In the history of this world there has never been a personality like you, who had such divine power to remove the clouds of atheism and impersonalism that were hanging over us. You are the greatest prophet of all time, and I can see clearly that you are the modern savior, hankered after by people in all parts of the world, sent to deliver mankind. Just by our presenting your invincible message, in our roles as your eternal servants and disciples, the hopelessness and ignorance of anyone, whether American, European, Oriental, Latin, African—-anyone, anywhere—can be permanently removed from his heart. The power of sun to remove the clouds and fog is insignificant next to you. I can’t begin to understand the extent of your power. But I am confident that it is more than enough to deliver the entire world from darkness and repeated death.

You once said that just as the individual soul cannot remain in his material body unless the Supersoul is there, so we could not remain in the temples and spiritual communities unless you were there. The enthusiasm of your ISKCON children and grandchildren all over the world is one of the proofs that you are very much present in ISKCON. You once instructed that whenever we get any money we should print as many books, in as many languages as possible, distribute them all over the world, and automatically this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement will expand. More than anywhere else, you are present within your books, and mass distribution of your books is like hundreds of millions of suns rising at once to attack the darkness. To distribute books require our special mercy, and the greatest mercy, and the greatest determination, so that we don’t allow anything to distract our minds from this mission even for a moment. You once said that wherever we go to present Kṛṣṇa consciousness we should display all your books; so we are making a new diorama of a complete set of all your books; which will be light enough for a preacher to take anywhere: on engagements, to collages, out on *saṅkīrtana* on our book or *prasādam* tables, etc. Over the past several years your ISKCON has begun printing in even more languages, and worldwide distribution has reached all-time highs. This year the first complete edition of the Chinese *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is* was offered at the Mayapur festival, and there was an ocean of transcendental emotion and loving feelings spontaneously shares among all your senior disciples who were present. I felt that the profound loving feelings pouring down on Tamāla Kṛṣṇa Maharaja represented your satisfaction at seeing this great achievement and that the wonderful ecstasy of the devotees was due to your being present within the *Bhagavat-gītā.* Otherwise how could we have felt so enthusiastic?

Over the past fifteen years you have personally build the greatest transcendental, worldwide missionary movement in the history of Kali-yuga. I feel that the only offering I can make to Your Divine Grace is to surrender myself to your plan and fully participate in this great International Society for Krishna Consciousness to enliven the conditioned souls to take up devotional service to Kṛṣṇa. We are actually nothing without your ever-present guidance. I pray that we may become so pure that at every moment we are conscious of you. I pray that we may be with you by living according to your instructions to awaken people to regularly chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa, seriously hearing Kṛṣṇa’s message (which you have brought), coming to our festivals, and taking *Kṛṣṇa-prasadam* with us. You once said that if we were pure, surrendered souls you could show us how to establish Kṛṣṇa consciousness in every country within eighteen days. I pray that our movement can be your pure instrument, acting under your guidance in these coming years to actually fulfill this vision—-Kṛṣṇa consciousness cities and countries in the 1980’s to offer to Your Divine Grace. That is our goal!

*Your unworthy servant,*  
Rāmeśvara Swami

## Rūpānuga dāsa

We, your humble disciples are, simply purporting what you have spoken. Our preaching is always a purport to what you have done or said or written. We read your books or hear your words and become mad with impatience to repeat them to everyone the best we can. But we have no independence. We may accept worship or credit up to the point of our own limited realization, but then we can only guide others to the shelter or your lotus feet. How can we give the pure love of Kṛṣṇa to anyone? How can we give what we do not have ourselves? We are completely subservient to you. You live in the ocean of pure love of God, and you are the giver of that love as well. Only what you deign to give us can we give to others.

You have left us, but we have kept you, deified you at the lotus feet of Nitāi-Gauranga, Sita-Rama, Lord Jagannatha, Kṛṣṇa-Balarama, Kṛṣṇa-Kaliya and Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa. The Lord is worshipped in different forms around the world, and you are deified as His eternal associate everywhere on this planet. You are the worshipable deity of every human being because you have saved the earth in the name of the Lord. Only what you deliver to us can we deliver to others.

No one can approach the six Gosvāmīs, Lord Caitanya or Lord Kṛṣṇa except through you, your disciples direct the love of others unto your lotus feet of all—for they have nothing to give in exchange for the love of others except your blessings. You are holding God’s love. Only what you choose to teach us can we teach to others.

You are the first worshipable deity of the world for everyone because you have brought Lord Kṛṣṇa outside of Bhārata-varṣa and distributed His love without prejudice for any class of men. What the world knows and will know of God they know by your grace. Only what you care to show us can we show to others.

You have taught your disciples how to take the Lords’s words literally and thus to see Him everywhere. Yet you are the one who romps with the Lord while we patiently await your blessings. Only what you wish to reveal to us can we reveal to others….

*Your most fallen disciple,*  
Rūpānuga dāsa

## Satsvarūpa Goswami

His Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda has informed us that there are two ways of associating with the spiritual master, the *vapuḥ,* or personal association, and the *vāṇī,* or the order of the spiritual master. Of the two, the *vāṇī* is especially important. One of the very important instructions of Śrīla Prabhupāda is that the devotees should associate, living and working together cooperatively in the International Society for Krishna Consciousness. Thus Prabhupada wrote in a letter of 1970, “Out ISKCON family, should be taken as a family based upon love and trust. So as the father becomes unhappy when one of his children wishes to break family connections, I also become unhappy when there are difficulties within our ISKCON family. So please do not consider leaving, as you are spiritual children of mine. Please chant your rounds sincerely and read all the books, and dedicate your life equally to Kṛṣṇa and spiritual master. This will make you happy.” At the very end of his life on this earth Śrīla Prabhupāda repeated the same message: “Your love for me will be shown by how you cooperate among yourselves after I have gone.” The difficulty in following this instruction was causing me and many devotees difficulty last year, and as the date came near for the annual Mayapur pilgrimage and G.B.C meeting, I was not completely confident about how to find the solution.

But going to Mayapur is another important instruction of yours, so naturally I complied. I can safely say, on behalf of all the devotees who attended this year’s Mayapur festival, that the following of your instruction to meet yearly in Mayapur proved this year a most wonderful success. As soon as I arrived at the Mayapur Chandrodaya Mandir, the sight of the beautiful garden and gigantic lotus fountain, so lovingly erected by your disciple Bhavānanda Goswami, made me feel like I had entered the spiritual planet, although I am unqualified to do so. Though I was a little skeptical, the association with my G.B.C Godbrothers soon worked its miraculous effect on my mind and heart, I am sorry to say that I made the least contribution to the meetings, and yet in retrospect I am feeling real appreciation for the hard work done all year round at these meetings also by your stalwart G.B.C men. In their company, I become purified, and realized that by working together, we would very easily conquer all obstacles.

And seeing all the devotees coming together from all parts of the world made me realize that the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is increasing. We can hardly keep track of all the new preaching centers that are opening in different parts of the world. Chanting together, working to preserve the principles of ISKCON, we sensed your presence. Thus, the G.B.C wrote in its paper prepared by the G.B.C subcommittee, “We have met this year in an inspired and decisive mood, and felt the unanimous confirmation of Prabhupada in our hearts.”

One year you expressed that the Mayapur pilgrimage should bring out the themes of unity in variety within ISKCON. You said that the variety was obvious, we have many different centers all around the world, and we are all different individuals. But the unity should also be stressed; that is, that we are all one under your direction, and within the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. So in Mayapur, our home away from our individual preaching centers, the assembled devotees felt strength and union. Now we are all back in our respective zones to do our duties, and whatever comes in the future we are dedicated and determined to serve you. And the ISKCON standard is clearly set by you. Men with shaven head, *śikhā* and *dhotī,* women in *sārīs,* all wearing *tilaka,* rising early in the morning, chanting, attending the morning program, *sannyāsis* traveling and preaching, householders reducing their needs and choosing the path of *nivṛtti-mārga,* all devotees combining to distribute your books and preach, under your order creatively developing new programs of *prasādam* distribution and financial support for book distribution and temple maintenance: all these guidelines have been set by you. Your instructions as you have left them are our guide, and there is no need to water them down. We are meant to be seen and heard in public, chanting and dancing, and behaving as gentlemen. We are meant to follow authority; the disciple follows the *guru,* and the Godbrother follows the G.B.C and temple president. We did these things in “old ISKCON” while you were here, and the present ISKCON is the same. Now, after associating with the devotees in Mayapur, I feel renewed confidence that those who go away from ISKCON or predict its weakening or doom are like dogs barking after the caravan. The dogs may bark, but the caravan will pass. And those who go away from ISKCON out of fear or laziness or faultfinding are not following your desires by any means.

There will always be discrepancies as long as we are not all completely pure devotees. As when you were present there were quarrels among the Godbrothers , so there are sometimes quarrels now. As when you were present sometimes a disciple would bloop and go away, so not it continues to happen. And as when you were present there was bad publicity, which you even said was a sign of our potency, so the bad publicity continues, and Kali-yuga “progresses.” But as you personally solved all problems by your orders in the past, so we can solve our present and future problems by submitting to your orders and the direction of G.B.C. Your orders and the orders of Lord Caitanya stand firm and eternal: in every town and village the chanting of Hare Kṛṣṇa will be known, and the Hare Kṛṣṇa movement, if pushed sincerely by its members, can introduce a golden age for the next ten thousand years.

So Śrīla Prabhupāda, I am feeling very hopeful. This is only due to your mercy. I pray that you allow me to go on describing and glorifying you in the biography form, serving you, my disciples, and Godbrothers, all in the peaceful house of ISKCON, and all by your grace. All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Satsvarūpa Goswami

## Tamāla Kṛṣṇa Gowami

**Prabhu-datta-deśa**

Eternal world The path of Bhakti  
Kṛṣṇa’s abode You did trace  
Message of Godhead Setting us each  
Still untold Within our place  
Who can deliver And to insure  
The human race? My prayerful penance,  
Mahāprabhu’s choice You chose a place  
Your Divine Grace. Of full dependence.

Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Where God’s name  
Gave to you Has no sound,  
The order of Nor transcendental thought  
Mahāprabhu. Be found;  
Divine mission Where jaded hearts  
All alone, Have built a wall  
We fallen souls Of void as truth—-  
To take home. I hear your call.

Ocean of mercy But I am blind,  
Humble and pure, How shall I know  
Our sinful pasts How to act  
You did endure Where to go?  
Fearless, bold In sympathy  
Empowered preacher, I set my heart.  
Peerless scholar Now your order  
Saintly teacher. Please impart.

Your purports shine Buddha’s children  
Within the night Gone astray  
Of maya’s kingdom Kṛṣṇa’s holy name  
Giving sight. Will pray.  
Faith supreme And Śrī Gauranga’s  
In Kṛṣṇa’s name, Holy will  
A spiritual world Shall Your Divine Grace  
Forged him from the mundane. Thus fulfill.

Tamāla Kṛṣṇa Goswami

## Vasudeva dāsa

*sādho jīvo vā māro vā*

A saintly person may either live or die, for during this life he is engaged in serving the Lord, and after death he serves the Lord as His eternal servant. Thus life and death are the same for a saintly person, for in both he serves the Lord constantly.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, not a single day is passed without remembering you; we think of you day and night. How fortunate we were to have had the rare opportunity to sit at your lotus feet and hear you speak, to speak with you personally and receive personal instructions from you occasionally. We are feeling deeply moved by these sweet remembrances. You are present in your books and instructions, but that does not satisfy us. Your physical absence is unbearable for us. By your causeless mercy you have pulled us out from this material entanglement, but we were not able to serve you directly in return. We are in your debt birth after birth.

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet, which alone can give shelter to the fallen souls. We beg for a drop of your mercy. *Jaya Prabhupada!*

*Your humble servant,*  
Vasudeva dāsa.

## Vyāsa-Pūjā Homages from ISKCON centers around the world

## Adelaide

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. Generally when I sit down to write the VYĀSA PŪJĀ homage for the temple I struggle with all kinds of philosophical points until finally as if I have simply complicated the whole matter.

This year, Śrīla Prabhupāda, I want to simply beg at your feet for the strength and direction to carry on my service to you. I am very much less intelligent and am completely attracted to the false pleasures of this material world, and therefore I am always straying into the dangerous realm of *māyā.*

Please give me shelter at your lotus feet, Śrīla Prabhupada, even though I have no qualification, for I have no shelter than your lotus feet.

You have made me take such a great responsibility, to always be in the best of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, to look after the devotees to give them an example to follow. Only if you give me your mercy is it possible for me to do these things, as your mercy is all that I am made of.

Please don’t think that I have lost my faith in you, Śrīla Prabhupāda; it is just that I am only a neophyte and am always in a bewildered state. In my heart of hearts I know that you love me, and I also love you, as you love all your disciples.

You are the most merciful, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and you are the most expert spiritual master, and therefore it is easily within your power to deliver an insignificant fool like me.

Please accept our prostrated obeisances at your divine feet.

*Your disciples and granddisciples in Adelaide, Australia.*

## Ahmedabad

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

We offer you most humble, worthless prostrations before our eternal savior, our preceptor and master, our heart and soul, our dearest Gurudeva, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Our obeisances to you again and again, thousands of time from the front and from behind and from all sides! It will be the most fortunate day in anyone’s life when he hears and understands your divine pastimes, your unbounded compassion for the suffering mankind, and your unflinching faith in the blessings of your spiritual master, the disciplic succession, Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa.

The last fifty years are considered the most significant in the development of science and technology in the world; and, as you have taught us, the more man has become dependent upon this technology and its comforts the more he has forgotten God and the more he has become demoniac. So fast have people forgotten their age-old customs of reading and hearing *Bhāgavata* discourses and *Mahābhārata* and *Rāmāyaṇa* even in this holy land of India. People have instead turned to cinemas and fiction and pornography. They make fun of someone who wants to be God conscious. Indeed the whole world has become a repository of *rajo-guna* and *tamo-guna,* as you have described in your poems written in the *Jaladuta.* Therefore, it is the greatest surprise for all the philosophers, religionists, and honest gentlemen how you so successfully established a spiritual movement based on purity of body, mind and the soul.

We were busy building up a house of false ego on the pillars of lust, anger, and greed. Intense desires were our bricks and ignorance was the cementing material. But you so graciously attacked us with the thunderbolt of Kṛṣṇa’s mercy, and the house of false ego was shattered to pieces before we could realize anything. But this mercy thunderbolt is distinct from the ordinary destructive thunderbolt because it brought forth the temples of joy for us to live in, inhabited by wonderful Godbrothers chanting and dancing, feasting and distributing love of God. You appeared all of a sudden in the darkness of Kali-yuga and brought in the rays of *Kṛṣṇa-prema* from Goloka Vṛndāvana. You illuminated the whole universe with Caitanyacandra rays; but, alas, the unfortunate Kali-yuga creature cannot yet understand this transcendental light. They are wondering, they are awestruck, they are baffled. The infinite number of *māyā’s* soldiers, with their latest equipment and ammunition, are also battled and badly defeated for the first time. You have caused a large defection in *māyā’s* army, a permanent, eternal defection—-a change of heart. Defection from darkness to light, from ignorance to knowledge, from threefold miseries to transcendental bliss, from lusty desires to *Kṛṣṇa-prema;* from hate, anger, selfishness to *vaisnava-prema.* How nice we feel to be saved! How nice is the taste of the nectar you have given us! How can we ever pay you back? Before we could even realize your mercy, before we could appreciate the benediction, you left us. All your disciples had just begun to give you a glimpse of assurance by printing books in thirty different languages, by opening up farm communities, by opening up temples in India, and so on. But we have yet to see a high court judge with *tilaka.* We have yet to see newspapers printing the spiritual news of Goloka and Vaikuntha rather than wars, murders, rapes, strikes, starvations, politics, and problems.

By your causeless mercy, people all over India are gradually realizing, at least partially, your greatness. Tens of thousands are becoming life members. Here in Gujerat we already have five million rupees worth of land in Baroda, and Surat. If you allow us, maybe your name will be a household word for generations to come. Maybe people will recognize your rightful position amongst the greatest *ācāryas* like Ramanuja, Madhvaśārya, Sankara. Maybe all the glowwormlike *bhogi-yogī svāmīs, avatāras,* and *bābā’s* will disappear when the sunrays of your name and fame will spread far and wide. Maybe *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is* will become a compulsory textbook for all students throughout the country. Maybe by your mercy people will worship, respect, and follow Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and Lord Nityananda rather than Nehrus and Gandhis.

We humbly pray on this auspicious day, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that you give us the strength to realize that you are always with us and that you are intently watching all our actions and that you enjoy and appreciate whatever little good we can bring for you and that you will rectify all our mistakes. Please give us the strength to miss your *vapuḥ* from and take more and more shelter of your *vāṇī* from. Please make us your fully surrendered puppets and make us carry on your mission of delivering the suffering humanity by making us your instruments.

*Your humble servants in ISKCON Gujerat,*

Yaśomatīnandana dāsa / Abhinanda dāsa / Vashghosa dāsa / Kṛṣṇa-nāma dāsa / Rūpa Goswami dāsa / Śukadeva dāsa / Jaya Jagadiśa dāsa / Rohiṇītanaya dāsa / Brahma-bhūta dāsa / Baṅkim Rāya dāsa / Nityānanda dāsa / Loka-bhandhu dāsa / Jagannātha-ratha-yātrā dāsa / Rājarṣi dāsa / Kārttika dāsa / Bhakta –Sharadendu / Bhakta Bankey / Bhakta Prakash / Bhakta Omprakash / Rādhā-kuṇdā-devī dāsī / Dharmakāla-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Sheela / Bhaktin Gayatri / Bhaktin Anila / Sundri / Nandu / Balārama / Rādhe

## Arequipa

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

On this glorious day of your appearing we only want to thank you because you have blessed us with your causeless mercy, giving us secure shelter at your lotus feet, which are like a cooling solace to all the living entities that suffer in the burning fire of material existence.

*Guru-Kṛṣṇa-Rūpa-hana śastrera prāmaṇe*  
*Guru-rūpe Kṛṣṇa-krpā karena bhakti-gaṇe*

**According to the opinion given in all the sacred scriptures, the spiritual master is not different from Kṛṣṇa. The Lord in the form of the spiritual master delivers His devotees.**

We are totally neophytes in devotional service, but we have faith that as a representative of the Supreme Personality of Godhead you have His qualities, especially His causeless mercy to the materially attached. This was always your mood in quietude and in anxiety: to think how you could deliver all the conditioned souls from the clutches of *māyā,* engaging them in devotional service. It is said in the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta:*

*tāte Kṛṣṇa bhaje, kare gurura sevana*  
*māyā jāla chuṭe pāya krsnera caraṇa*

**“If the conditioned soul engages in the service of the Lord and simultaneously carries out the orders of his spiritual master and serves him, he can get shelter at Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet.”**

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, accept us eternally as your servants and let us be always engaged in service at your lotus feet.

Please give us strength and determination because we ourselves haven’t any qualification. If you don’t bless us with your grace the our lives will be lost.

By the grace of the spiritual master, the cloud of Kṛṣṇa’s mercy is attracted and them, when the rain of Kṛṣṇa consciousness falls, the fire of material existence is extinguished.

*Your fallen servants in Arequipa Mandira,*

Abhimanyu-suta dāsa / Vāsudeva-parāyaṇa dāsa / Aṣtavarga dāsa / Haraye dāsa / Bhakta Alex / Bhakta Roberto / Bhakta Wilmer / Bhakta Crisofolo / Maṇimānyārikā-devī dāsī

## Argentina

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet, and let us take shelter there eternally. In Argentina the preaching of Kṛṣṇa consciousness has been banned by the government, the devotees have been persecuted, and your books confiscated from individuals when found. Still the preaching is going on, and by Your Divine Grace people are coming forward; we are getting facilities, and there is a lot of hope for the future.

Actually, the apparent difficulties in Argentina become insignificant when we remember what *you* went through to give *us* Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Having struggled so much to print the First Canto of the *Śrīmad-Bhagavatam,* and to arrange your journey to America, being discouraged by others not to go, with such an old and fragile body, still you sailed on the *Jaladuta,* risking your own life, to come to the West.

Once here, without friends or money, your fought right and left to establish a preaching program, finding that you had to preach to an audience of monkey and similar wild species. Still you convinced us to chant Hare Kṛṣṇa and to do some little devotional service to Śrī Kṛṣṇa. In the midst of all this you were able to produce the *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is,* the *Srimad-Bhagavatam,* the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta,* etc., organized the development of our movement all over the world, and maintained the spiritual lives of thousands who intelligently took shelter at your lotus feet.

So this remembrance of you is all that we need to develop the strength to succeed in our preaching programs. Please protect and guide us so that we can serve you forever.

*Your servants in Argentina.*

## Athens

Who is he whose heart is always more joyful, radiating hopefulness and compassion? Where is he who can give us relief and assurance in this dark and oppressive atmosphere? We grope but are disappointed. We hope to be hopeful, but the horizon is empty; sunrise becomes sunset and darkness, and each day passes offering only emptiness and collapsing promises. Why do we search? Because we have lost someone most dear and we cannot forget. Although frustration and disappointment are without end we still wait, and pray, for the one whose heart will give us all life again; we long for the one whose heart longs for our eternal peace; we ache in expectation of one whose heart aches while seeing our endless groping. We cry because we need his living shelter.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**“We offer our most respectful obeisances unto His Diving Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupada, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa having taken shelter at His lotus feet. Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvati Gosvami. You are so kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.”**

**“O all-merciful spiritual master, representative of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, when will \[our\] minds be completely surrendered unto your lotus feet? At that time only, by your mercy, I shall be able to get relief from all obstacles to spiritual life, and I shall be situated in blissful life.” (*****Kṛṣṇa,*** **Vol. 3, p. 177: Śrīpada Srīdhara Svami)**

The child, while learning to speak, is unable to do more than babble, and yet the father is very, very pleased to hear such endeavors. So, in the same situation we pray to our beloved spiritual master, His Divine Grace Abhaya Carana Bhaktivedanta Swami Maharaja Prabhupada on this most auspicious day of his appearance, Śrī VYĀSA PŪJĀ, that we as his insignificant disciples and granddisciples can give him some pleasure although our attempt to glorify him is nothing more than childish babbling.

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, just as the ocean is the cause of the clouds and of the rivers which flow back again into the ocean itself, so also you are the cause of our liberation and of the ability we have to glorify you.

So many people claim to know the *Bhagavad-gītā,* but only your followers can practice Lord Kṛṣṇa’s teachings. We wanted to live lives of harmony, value and purpose, but only by your guidance could we achieve that result. By the mercy of the ocean, the rivers nourish the countryside, fields and orchards; in the same way, by your oceanic compassion, we are able to relieve, nourish and cure our fellow men, who are lying lifeless on the dry ground of material existence. And just as the rivers flow again into the ocean, so our appreciation and glorification of you strengthens our relationship and brings others closer to you.

By the reciprocation of service(by the disciple to the spiritual master) and of blessings( by the spiritual master upon the disciple) all transcendental fruits are enjoyed. Kṛṣṇa states in the *Bhagavad-gītā,* that only by pure devotional service can He be known as He is. Similarly, only by such service can one know the spiritual master, the external manifestation of the Supersoul, Kṛṣṇa within the heart. Kṛṣṇa confirms this in the *Śrīmad-Bhagavatam* where He says that the pure devotee spiritual master must be accepted and respected as His very Self. Also, *Caitanya-caritamrta* describes the bona fide spiritual master as the Supreme Personality of Servitor Godhead. Therefore the relationship between the spiritual master and the disciple depends on the reciprocation of service and benediction between the two. Only as such s he is able to accept, follow and serve the transcendental desires of the *guru* can the disciple relish the transcendental aspects of his own spiritual personality as well as sublime mellows of loving relationship with the spiritual master.

Because materialistic persons stubbornly reject the chain of disciplic succession, they cannot enter into these mysteries, and they foolishly continue to act in ignorant, restricted, and destructive ways. They condemn the path of *bhakti,* but they are unable to give up unwanted activity (what to speak of being able to help others.) They are forced to act helplessly, under the dictation of their senses and mind, whereas the devotees are free from this restriction. They can see that the devotees are free, but they close their eyes very proudly and declare that they also are free.

To resolve this dangerous situation, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray to you for your continued benediction of strength to preach your message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness without fear. We see the world awakening to spiritual consciousness by the guidance of your empowered representatives. The doubts of the general mass of innocent people are being driven away by the effulgent explanations of your surrendered disciples. They have taken your banner and are successfully defeating all the atheistic and skeptical challenges to the fact of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s inconceivable potency and mercy, and His supreme dominion over all that be.

We have no doubt of your perfect position and message, Śrīla Prabhupāda, but being only babbling, immature and self-interested children we have not achieved our actual position of complete dependence upon you. Therefore we are not yet steady or perfect in our service of glorification of Your Divine Grace. So please allow us to follow in the footsteps of Śrīla Sanātana Goswami Maharaja in offering this humble prayer at your most merciful lotus feet.

“By your grace \[we\] have appreciated the conclusions which you have taught \[us\], but we are so low that we cannot even touch a drop of the ocean of your instructions. Thus, if you want \[us\], who are nothing but lame men, to dance, then please give us your benediction by placing your feet on \[our\] heads.”

Again, accept our most respectful and grateful obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

*All your servants, disciples and granddisciples at Athens Yatra.*

## Atlanta

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

All glories to Your Divine Grace, our eternal well-wisher and father. Dear master, in your absence the world is a fearful, dark place, devoid of love and protection. Only by taking shelter of your instructions can we feel secure in this world. We remember your last visit to Atlanta in 1975; you told us then that you were pleased by the spirit of cooperation your disciples showed in working together to serve you. You explained that just as the British Empire had succeeded in circling the globe because of their spirit of cooperation, similarly the secret of our success in spreading Lord Caitanya’s movement and building your transcendental society would be to maintain that same spirit of cooperation.

Although we do not deserve your mercy, please bless us that our hearts will become softened and we can learn to fully love and appreciate each and every ISKCON devotee and cooperate nicely, as you desired.

Until you came, I was one more fool at play  
Like a blind man claiming that he knows the way.  
Every day was just another wasted day  
Spent in talking, with nothing to say.

Covered up with ignorance so deep and dark;  
The seed you planted, it gave the world its spark,  
And with the torch of knowledge, you caused my eyes to see  
Kindly restoring my true identity.

With you came, teaching truth that never dies;  
You were absolute, you would not compromise.  
You wrote it down on paper, you wrote it on our hearts  
That we must serve the whole, we are all His parts.

By your mercy, I could see on the horizon  
Winter had gone and the lovely spring had returned.  
Lifting the veil of fear and sorrow, you revealed that tomorrow  
Is dependent on today’s lessons learned.

*Your servants in Pānnihāṭi Dhāma*

## Auckland

So many poets and so many poems  
In this material world  
But what have they done?  
So much of every human being  
And each and every one  
Is thinking that what he’s doing  
Is the absolute all in all,  
But compared to you Śrīla Prabhupāda  
They all appear so small.

I mean, I’m not very poetic  
Though I try to be at times  
I tend to lose devotion  
Thinking more of what word rhymes,  
But everything you wrote was just  
Pure poetry to my ears  
I don’t think I could copy you  
If I had a thousand years.

Your appearance was so regal  
In a dignified special way,  
A certain unmistakable excellence  
Even few great men portray,  
A definite deep and real awareness  
Of what matters most in life,  
You were always clear and calm  
Through your trouble and strife.

So proud you looked as well I think  
As you held your head up high;  
People would often show respect  
To see you pass them by;  
Your pride of course was not for you,  
Your pride was for the Lord;  
Your humbleness was obvious  
You wanted no reward.

I remember too your morning walks  
With cane and beads in hand,  
You’d preach to us so strongly  
That we might understand  
How serious is your mission  
And important is our task;  
You’d even answer questions  
We would be too dull to ask.

I loved the way you answered  
After lectures that you gave;  
Some crazy man would start up  
On some speculative rave;  
“So who is God?” you’d challenge  
and then joke at his reply;  
There was none who could defeat you,  
It was fun to see them try.

Your psychology was perfect,  
You knew everybody’s heart;  
When sane men came with fools  
You could tell them apart,  
and if someone tried to cheat you  
his motive you would find,  
just by looking at his face  
you could understand his mind.

I loved as well your japa  
With your deep resounding voice;  
Even now I’m drawn to listen  
As if I have no choice;  
“Sit properly,” I hear you say,  
and know it’s meant for me,  
for seated means with Kṛṣṇa  
and proper means purity.

I’ve always loved your kīrtana  
Since the very first time I heard it;  
Although I like all music  
Your voice, I much preferred it;  
I often wish that others now  
Be drawn to hear you sing  
Your songs of love for Kṛṣṇa  
Which can all perfection bring.

Yes this poem is just a glimpse  
Of a real pure devotee;  
Your glories should be manifest  
For all the world to see,  
And although I know that personally  
I can’t do much myself  
By Your Divine Grace, your books I’ll see  
On every karmīs shelf.

*Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda, ki jaya.*

*Your servants at Auckland Yatra,*

## Bali

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept my most humble and insignificant obeisances at your lotus feet. I have been trying without success to write something which is worthy of you. My words are all empty statements because my heart is shallow. In Vṛndāvana sometimes we would sit and talk with you about Kṛṣṇa, the cowherd boys, Mother Yaśodā, Nanda Maharaja, the *gopīs* and the cows. It was very simple, and I could understand all that and that you were one of them. It wasn’t very complicated, but it was deep with love. I just remember you in Vṛndāvana, and my mind is flying away.

*Your aspiring servants at Bali Yatra,*
