## Chandigarh

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances. Because you directly represent Kṛṣṇa, who is beyond the limit of the material designation, you also are free from any such condition. From the spiritual world you are desiring that your ISKCON shall remain strong, dynamic and united, and so it must. You’ve stated. “You’ll show your love for me by how you work together cooperatively.” This instruction is forcing us to be pure, to give up personal ambition and desire for sense gratification. Obliging us to work for the good of the society as a whole, even if it means accepting a lowly position. Helping us to be prideless, not to seek the satisfaction of being honored by others.

This is your ISKCON. Please allow us to remain connected to it. If we are separated we become *asāra,* useless. Help us to remember you in the difficult moments, when our Godbrothers irritate us, when the *karmīs* antagonize us. Give us the strength to push on this great movement of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Benedict us with your full mercy so that we can overcome all obstacles on the path of devotion and become actually qualified to represent you.

*We beg to remain your insignificant servants,*

Nava Yogendra Swami / Trivikrama Swami / Lakśmīśvara dāsa brahmacari / Dadhi-bhakṣa dāsa brahmacari / Madana-mohana dāsa brahmacari / Sunanda dāsa brahmacari / Advaita dāsa brahmacari / Nārāyaṇa dāsa brahmacari / Pūtanāri dāsa brahmacari / Bhakta Arun / Bhakta Bhanadari / Bhakta Hari Singh / Bhakta Prem Natha / Mr. & Mrs. D.D. Sharma / Mr. & Mrs. L.S.Thakur / Mr. & Mrs. Lalit Gupta

## Chicago

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

All glories to you. By your causeless mercy, you once traveled all over the surface of this planet leaving the dust of your lotus feet. That is such a purifying substance, that to put it on our lowly heads is the supreme benediction. You have personally blessed many places by your walking and talking for and about the Supreme Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, and Chicago is one of them. Now you have come and gone in one sense yet continue to remain in a deeper, transcendental way. We also remain, your disciples, and now granddisciples, as instruments to fulfill your desires. Actually your desire has brought us into being, and working to fulfill your desire continues our life. In this way we live with Your Divine Grace, not at all in Chicago. Chicago is in fact far from our minds, and we think of it only as a place where unfortunate persons have not yet heard of your glories. We met you at O’Hare airport in 1975. I remember how you appeared from the airplane, astounding so many ordinary persons. Here was the man. “It’s him, Hare Kṛṣṇa. That’s the guy.” But quickly their whole concept of Kṛṣṇa consciousness altered as loving disciples cried and bowed and chanted and danced, “*Jaya* Prabhupada,” and you smiled, glancing here and there, not whimsically, but in benediction, and everyone knew it. Garlanded with flowers, surrounded with devotees. It is not possible to describe such a vision, what to speak of the satisfied, ecstatic minds of your disciples who knew no greater security. Now Prabhupada was here. Nothing else mattered. Serving you even in the most insignificant way was the only thing that had any relevance. Personally I felt fully protected; yet there were so many demons always harassing us in attempting to fulfill your order of book distribution. Walking alongside your transcendental form, we approached the exit. I wanted to go ahead and step in the mat to open the door. But as I started to move, we were there, and you raised only slightly your arm in a gesture, and I felt brushed aside like an insignificant insect. And you walked on fully protected by your desire to preach. Later at the apartment arranged for your stay, you talked of “crime and what to do.” That was one of the main themes of your preaching. Meeting with a police lieutenant and the press as well as your disciples, you explained how we could cure the problem of crime in America. I wanted to go immediately to every prison and establish our *saṅkīrtana* movement. In fact, whatever you talked about I immediately wanted to do for you. In this way, during your presence I was completely confused and accomplished nothing but managed to think of you constantly. Śrīla Prabhupāda, your preaching theme in Chicago during your visit continues to be the main theme now. It’s a crime that although you have given us everything, our offering to you is so paltry. So what to do? How can we repay you and please you? Our hearts are very cold and hard. This is a crime, that we don’t take your instruction, your very self, to heart and melt our hearts, as yours is melted in love for Kṛṣṇa. Please accept this humble offering as an attempt to stop this crime once and for all and inspire us, embarrass us by your kindness to give up any desire other than yours.

*Your servants,*

Tripurāri Swami / Mṛgendra dāsa brahmacari / Kalki dāsa brahmacari / Śrī Nātha dāsa brahmacari / Bala dāsa brahmacari / Rāmaī dāsa brahmacari / Aindra dāsa brahmacari / Ruci dāsa brahmacari / Bāṇabhaṭṭa dāsa brahmacari / Pṛthuśravā dāsa brahmacari / Prasāda dāsa brahmacari / Bhavānanda Raya dāsa brahmacari / Govinda Datta dāsa brahmacari / Sarvopama dāsa brahmacari / Vaiśeṣika dāsa brahmacari / Kṣamābhuddhi dāsa brahmacari / Cakrī dāsa brahmacari / Jitendriya dāsa brahmacari / Garuḍa dāsa adhikārī / Śikhi Māhiti dāsa adhikārī / Gaura-devī dāsī / Haripriyā-devī dāsī / Ananta-devī dāsī / Sāṅga-devī dāsī / Bhakta Jim / Bhakta Deva / Bhakta Rod / Bhakta Kevin / Bhaktin Kevin / Bhaktin Bill / Bhaktin Careem / Bhaka Tim / Bhakta Micheal / Bhaktin Nalini / Bhaktin Shelly / Bhaktin Biwan

## Christchurch

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
This age is so difficult, tis fraught with distress.  
We’re prone to illusion, the whole world’s in a mess.  
But Kṛṣṇa’s all kind, He doesn’t want us to suffer,  
He sent you to guide us, you’re our only shelter.  
You’re the beacon of light to whom we must be near,  
As ships tossed on the ocean chart courses and steer.  
O Śrīla Prabhupāda, O merciful master,  
The movement you started is saving the world.  
It’s amazing to witness your ISKCON banner unfurl.  
We are all simply instruments in your divine hands,  
May we all be of use to you in fulfilling your plans.  
We know you want Kṛṣṇa worshiped in every home,  
With your books as the basis to be everywhere known.  
Chanting and dancing, the cures guaranteed,  
For all the ills of the Kali age you’ve fulfilled the great need.  
You gave the world temples where Kṛṣṇa can stay,  
Being served by your servants, so as never to stray.  
Wonderful farms you made fruitful by protecting the cow,  
Simple living and high thinking, we feel satisfied now.  
Guru-kula for all children irrespective of caste, color, or creed,  
You desire that they hear about Kṛṣṇa in this hour of need.  
In your empowered representatives the ISKCON aśāryas,  
We see you still present, so pure are their characters.  
So much more you have done for us, O compassionate lord,  
Eternal service to you is our treasured reward.  
In separation from you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, our hearts are now sore,  
We humbly pray for the chance to see you once more.  
In reading your books we find consolation,  
Till the time you again bless us with your eternal association.

*Your grateful servants at ISKCON Christchurch,*

Alanātha dāsa / Parivādī dāsa / Bhakta Terry / Viṣṇupādī-devī dāsī / Rukmiṇī-devī dāsī / Jānakī-devī dāsī / Sunīti-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Patrice

## Cleveland

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

By your causeless mercy you have allowed everyone desiring it to get the mercy of Kṛṣṇa, especially the most fallen conditioned souls. Here in Cleveland we have been blessed with the service of Śrī-Śrī Rādhā-Muralīdhara and are expertly guided by you through your pure disciple Kīrtanānanda Swami. Through your limitless kindness you have inspired and encouraged every one of your disciples to engage in Kṛṣṇa’s service. Like a touchstone, *cintāmani,* you have changed everyone to your influence, the more realization of Kṛṣṇa has manifested within our hearts. Your divine association is purifying as you appear to us in your words and form. You have always been the perfect example of an *aśārya.*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have often appeared just like a parent patiently caring for his helpless children, correcting our mistakes, and showing us the proper way to advance in spiritual life. At the same time, you have given us the most rare and intimate association with Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, in Their most personal forms and *līlās.* You have given us Vṛndāvana everywhere and the power to transform this entire world into Vaikuntha. You have unfolded and revealed the spiritual world before us, unworthy as we are. You have given us endless devotional service to perform. You have given us the worship of the holy names. But most of all you have given us the opportunity to sit at your lotus feet.

We are eternally indebted to your kindness as we have not yet begun to realize your greatness or glorify you in a befitting way. Please forgive our countless offenses and please do not kick us away. Without you we are eternally hopeless.

As you are always with your devotee who is thinking of you, may we constantly have your association.

*The devotees of New Govardhana Hill,*

Sthñi-bhāva dāsa / Veda-pravartaka dāsa / Kṛṣṇa-śravaṇam dāsa / Rāma Gopāla dāsa / Cakrapāṇi dāsa / Dīna-vatsala dāsa / Hari Murāri dāsa / Mahā-mantra dāsa / Bhakti-yoga dāsa / Līlā-smaraṇam dāsa / Līlāśuka dāsa / Bhakta Śyāmasundara / Bhakta Viśvambhara / Bhakta Goloka / Bhakta Vrajānanda / Bhakta Rāmānanda / Bhakta William / Bhakta Larry / Bhakta Jim / Tulasī-mañjarī-devī dāsī / Kāmagiri-devī dāsī / Kadamba-devī dāsī / Kṛṣṇa-śakti-devī dāsī / Jāhnavī-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Pat / Bhaktin Madana-mohana

## Columbus

Glorious indeed my brothers,  
In all its glory let it be chimed throughout the land,  
The blessed event, the momentous hour  
The appearance of His Divine Grace.  
Our life’s true fortune is at hand.

Truly in darkness and delusion  
Did we the face of this earth roam.  
In ignorance deluded and fallen  
No memory, not a trace of what we all hoped in some form—-  
That certainly at the end of our journey  
There must certainly be some place we could call home.

In all sincerity  
The message of our Gurudeva  
Is the undeniably crystaly clear utmost clarity;  
For not one moment should we ever again forget  
His heart of molten love of the Lord our God  
To be our only life and breath.

His example impeccable, outstanding, and profound,  
The ocean of us all conditioned souls  
With his divine demeanor did astound,  
Rejuvenated and resurrected now  
We were all practically dead;  
Take the unimaginable opportunity, nectar of nectars,  
Keep the appearance of the feet of our beloved master  
Ever on our head.

Now and forever and at the hour of our death,  
Never tiring, ever awakening like the sun  
The harkings of daylight, his presence in our midst  
The greatest boon ever known to man  
The truth, the way and the light, the veritable crest.  
Obeisances and obeisances again and again  
From all sides  
Your appearance the mercy to  
This world at last!  
All of us who are suffering or alone in need  
Have a savior unto whom to hold fast  
The truth of life, Kṛṣṇa consciousness,  
The fixed path  
Your strong unwavering stance and conviction  
The necessary proclamation to eternal life in divinity  
Beyond chance.

The gift of yourself will ever be  
The greatest gift of all,  
Worship Your Divine Grace in every turn of life,  
The doubtless remedy  
New life and health,  
Refreshed and recharged  
The cure for us all.

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances. Our desire is that we remember you at each moment of each day just as we’re remembering you now. Were it not for Your Divine Grace, we would still simply be chewing the chewed, and full of frustration and anxiety. Yet out of your causeless mercy, through your divine instructions you have opened to us the transcendental realm, which is ever-increasing and unlimited.

No one but you took the time or had the care or even had the ability to teach us how to serve Kṛṣṇa and to chant His holy name. You are the ideal *ācārya,* and wherever you have been or wherever you may go in the three worlds, your presence invokes all auspiciousness. There is no possibility of us expressing the debt of gratitude that we owe you.

We pray for your mercy that somehow you will enable us to preach vigorously as you have done, knowing this to be the only way to really please you.

Please give us eternally the shelter of your divine lotus feet.  
*The devotees of the Columbus temple*

## Croome Court

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

We humbly submit our fallen selves before you on this most auspicious occasion of your VYĀSA PŪJĀ day and pray that you allow us to continue serving you although we are totally unqualified. Without you, there would be no ISKCON, no temples, no *saṅkīrtana* movement, no devotees, and no *bhakti* in the Western world. It is quite hopeless for us to imagine what this world would be like without the benediction rays of your mercy that you so readily bestowed upon the most wretched members of human society who by your inconceivable potency were transformed into exalted Vaisnavas within a span of a few short years. It is said that one may judge a good father by his good sons, such as in the case of Maharaja Ṛṣabhādeva, Arjuna, and others. You have excelled in producing so many first-class sons, who, in turn are fathering so many first-class sons, your grandsons. Thus your lineage is perpetuated and will continue to be so for thousands of years. We can understand that this movement is only functioning by your command and expanding by your desire. Your vision stretches far into the future, and your instructions are meant for all men at all times. You are the expert architect who has laid down the plans for a house large enough for all societies in this world to live in peacefully and harmoniously. You have provided us with the pure ingredients for a perfect diet to sustain real human life, namely, one *mantra—-*Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rama, Hare Rama, Rama Rama, Hare Hare; one scripture, *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is;* one work, devotional service to Kṛṣṇa. You have instructed us to distribute the foodstuffs of this diet to Kṛṣṇa. You have instructed us to distribute the foodstuffs of this diet to everyone we meet, putting an end to spiritual starvation once and for all. Please allow us to remain as your menial servants for fulfilling your mission of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world.

*Your most unworthy servants,*  
The residents of Chaitanya College at Croome Court,  
Worcester, England

## Cuzco

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

It is very difficult to accept, for someone with a materialistic heart like me, that you had been on this planet a short time ago. We can say that this was yesterday, and still I can’t know you. It is very difficult, my dear master, for someone like me to understand that you had accepted me as your disciple without ever seeing me and that you have taken me out of this horrible black well that is this material world. And that you have taken me now under your transcendental care.

It is very difficult for someone who is covered grossly by material concepts like me to understand in this moment all the fortune that has come to my hands since the light of your mercy touched me in the middle of a stormy night.

It is very difficult for someone who is accustomed to taste the inauspiciousness and the atrophied, like me, to enjoy the eternal and ambrosial nectar of your instructions, your pastimes, your personality, your wisdom, and your transcendental association, which creates the most elevated pleasure for your pure devotees.

And it is very difficult, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, for a fool like me to understand you. But in spite of my inability, still I want to pray like this, prostrated at your lotus feet, and with all humbleness, that you will please never throw me out of the association of your devotees and give me just one opportunity to execute my service in a form that can satisfy you.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, I fall at your lotus feet like a useless servant.

Vāsudeva-parāyaṇa dāsa / Bhagavan Ācārya dāsa / Ṭoṭa-Gopīnātha dāsa / Haṁsa dāsa / Hari-caraṇa dāsa / Rājā-guhyam dāsa / Bhakta Anibal / Bhakta Juan / Bhakta Hugo / Abhidheyā-devī dāsī / Śasīmātā-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Maria

## Dallas

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances on this most auspicious eighty-fifth anniversary of your appearance within this material world. Nearly sixteen years have passed since you first gazed upon the bleak Kali-yuga landscape on Boston Harbor. This great moment in history, September 18, 1965, went unnoticed by the sleepwalking conditioned souls of twentieth-century America. But you had come, Śrīla Prabhupāda, the emissary of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu to deliver the fallen *mlecchas* of the West. You had entered just like a needle, but in twelve short years you had plowed the field and planted the seeds of this world-wide Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are eternally indebted to you. On the order of your spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvati Gosvami Prabhupada, you have come as Lord Caitanya’s ambassador to this barbaric place. Just as an ambassador from a mighty empire must undergo great austerities in executing the emperor’s orders, so you have undergone great personal hardships while spreading Lord Caitanya’s Mahāprabhu’s *saṅkīrtana* movement all over the world.

In your own words you have said, “Most of the population here is covered by the material modes of ignorance and passion. Absorbed in material life they think themselves very happy and satisfied, and therefore, they have no taste for the transcendental message of Vasudeva (Kṛṣṇa).” Thus, we can understand that you have accomplished an impossible feat, for you have turned us poor souls—-“covered by the material modes of ignorance and passion \[and \]absorbed in material life”—-into Vaisnavas.

The sound of *karatālas* and the *mṛdaṅga* is familiar in cities all over the world, while your Bhaktivedanta Purports, the scriptures for the Kali-yuga, are to be found in millions of homes. In this way, you have spread Kṛṣṇa’s name to every town and village on this planet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are *jagad-guru,* and this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is spreading simply by your mercy. We pray that we may take shelter at your lotus feet and be permitted to assist you in your eternal mission to deliver the fallen souls within this material world.

*Your eternal servants of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kālacandjī Dhāma,*

## Delaware

*Dear Godbrothers and Godsisters,*

Please accept our humble obeisances at your transcendental lotus feet. On this, the most worshipable appearance day of His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupada, we, as his disciples and eternal servants, are taking the opportunity to step forward and attempt to offer prayers for his glorification.

Śrīla Prabhupāda is indeed the true spiritual master, for he is revealing to us our eternal identity as spirit souls and as eternal servants of the supreme soul, the transcendental Personality of Godhead, Kṛṣṇa. Prabhupada’s every word is simply meant to evoke our dormant love for God. He never spoke to draw attention to himself, to acquire wealth, fame, or adoring followers. He wrote volume upon volume of books glorifying Kṛṣṇa, the all-attractive Supreme, and teaching all human beings to do the same. This total selflessness completely distinguishes Śrīla Prabhupāda from all so-called *gurus, svāmīs,* and spiritual masters.

And yet, although he did not want any praise, there is no one more worthy of praise then he. This is not just our private sentiment as Śrīla Prabhupāda’s disciples; it is a practical fact. Prabhupada is the most praiseworthy personality, and we are prepared to explain this fact to everyone.

First of all, Kṛṣṇa is more pleased when we glorify Śrīla Prabhupāda, His pure devotee, then when we try to glorify Kṛṣṇa directly—-just as a loving father is most pleased with the praise offered to his favorite son. Kṛṣṇa is the father of everyone, but He does not care for the prayers of persons who are inimical to His pure devotees and who are not accustomed to offering humble service at their feet. In the *Ādi Purāṇa* Lord Kṛṣṇa Himself says, “My dear Partha, one who claims to be My devotee is not so. Only a person who claims to be the devotee of My devotee is actually My devotee.

Secondly, Kṛṣṇa explains in the *Bhagavad-gītā* that it is impossible for men, either individually or by collective planning, to escape the suffering of material life. Everyone must suffer old age, disease and death, and no amount of advancement in any field of material science can ever alter this fact. But Kṛṣṇa also explains that by surrendering to Him we can leave this world of suffering altogether and return to our eternal life of bliss and knowledge in the spiritual world. “Eternal life,” “bliss,” “knowledge,” “spiritual world”—-the suffering materialist thinks that these are all empty phrases. But Śrīla Prabhupāda, as the perfect example of complete surrender to Kṛṣṇa, filled all sincere souls with the faith that these are not at all meaningless phrases, and with the determination to themselves surrender, following his example and his instructions. Śrīla Prabhupāda is the object of praise for truly intelligent men because he showed everyone how to end all misery by serving Kṛṣṇa in full surrender. Anyone who can hear what Śrīla Prabhupāda has spoken, read what he has written, or see what he has accomplished, and still have no faith in Kṛṣṇa and the spiritual world must have an intelligence no better than the intelligence of dogs, hogs, and asses.

So although he never desired any recognition for himself, Śrīla Prabhupāda is the most worthy of recognition. We have here given only two reasons for this. But there are not one or two, but an infinite number of reasons for glorifying our beloved spiritual master. He is so great that as soon as we start to speak, our glorification appears crude and insufficient, and we are forced to speak on and on, ever trying to do him justice. This is our happy predicament. So let us all spend our lives eternally in this way, enumerating the glories of His Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda and introducing others to the process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, the process which leads us back to our eternal life of bliss and knowledge in the spiritual world.

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your servants at ISKCON Delaware*

## Denmark

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept my obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. Because you are incessantly surcharged by the eternal embrace of Lord Caitanya, you are the storehouse of compassion and mercy for the *jīvas* disconnected from normal life with the Supreme Personality of Godhead. As each year passes, since your physical departure, the pastimes, accomplishments, and fame of your saving power tower higher and higher. Yet still I, dull-headed and foggy, am shocked at how even an insignificant, inconsequential disciple like me has a chance to bathe a little in the warm and happy ocean of your personal glance and care.

Though I always fervently articulated the appropriate philosophy, inwardly I thought I was hopelessly finished, never to obtain a taste of your ambrosial association. This agonizing burden of poison was my destiny, I thought, to struggle under the rest of this life.

But because you are Prabhupada, of course, the spiritual master at whose feet all other spiritual masters sit, because you are the paragon of a bona fide *guru,* you always knew, *daivī hy eṣā guṇamayī mama māyā duratyayā:* “This silly boy will not get out of material existence until he develops love for me.” How that will happen I am now sure you will arrange. I am completely helpless and dependent on you, even to know and love you, my eternal father, my ruling master, and my most precious well-wisher.

As the first scents of your lotuslike regal existence waft throughout this world, entire nations are beginning to shake. This I have personal experience of . Later, when Your Divine Grace firmly strides, with all your transcendental paraphernalia and associates, into the hearts and constitutions of all men, and when the heads of bowing government leaders become humble, submissive cushions for your imperial lotus feet, then the world will finally spin in its normal condition. My duty is to assist in your accomplishing this task, and I beg to never lose sight of your service and objectives.

Devāmṛta dāsa / Keśināśana dāsa / Prema-rasa dāsa / Janeśvara dāsa / Daśāvatāra dāsa / Payonidhi dāsa / Bhakta Peter / Bhakta Ayo / Bhakta Ryszard / Bhakta Lars / Bhakta Claus / Bhakta Neils / Bhakta Conni / Bhaktin Mette / the devotees of ISKCON Poland

## Denver

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. I offer my respectful obeisances unto my spiritual master, the deliverer of the fallen souls. By his mercy a dumb man turns into an eloquent speaker, and he also enables the lame to cross over mountains. Without his shelter the whole world is wandering in the ocean of ignorance.

After receiving the seed of devotional service from the bona fide *guru,* one had to cultivate the creeper by the watering process, which is to chant the *maha-nantra:* Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rama, Hare Rama, Rama Rama, Hare Hare. As the main creeper of devotional service begins to grow, one has to take special caution to check any weeds which may try to kill the growth of the creeper. These weeds will appear in the form of the ten offenses to the holy name.

While this watering process is going on, one must inquire from the spiritual master on matters regarding devotional service, and one must render all kinds of service to him also. This will provide good ground so that the creeper may spread its roots. All that remains is the sun to nourish the creeper. Thus it is by your causeless mercy that we are able to nourish the creeper. Thus it is by your causeless mercy that we are able to make the advancement in Kṛṣṇa consciousness because you appear to us just like the shining sun.

From this we can understand that only when one comes in contact with a bona fide spiritual master is it possible to receive the seed of devotion. We hear through *śāstra* that the living entity, according to his *karma,* is wandering through the universe in the upper and lower planetary system, acquiring so many gross bodies. Out of these millions of living entities, one who is very fortunate gets the opportunity to associate with a bona fide spiritual master by the grace of Kṛṣṇa.

Guru-Kṛṣṇa-rūpa hana śāstrera pramāṇe  
Guru-rūpe kṛpā karena bhakti gaṇe

According to all revealed scriptures you are actually nondifferent from Kṛṣṇa, who comes as the spiritual master to deliver His devotees.

It is also stated in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (1.1.22):

*Tvaṁ naḥ sandarśito dhātrā*  
*Dustaraṁ nistitīrṣatām*  
*Kaliṁ sattva-haraṁ puṁsāṁ*  
*Karṇa-dhāra ivārṇavam*

**“We think that we have met Your Grace by the will of providence, and thus we accept you as the captain of the ship for those who desire to cross the dangerous ocean of Kali, which destroys all the good qualities of a human being.”**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are *jagad-guru,* enacting this *saṅkīrtana* mission right here in this most degraded culture. The people here are of the lowest caliber of *mlecchas* and *śūdras.* And you, our divine master, have left your beloved Goloka Vṛndāvana to come and engage them properly in Kṛṣṇa’s service.

For all the knowledge and happiness that you are constantly showering upon us, all you ask in return is for us to help you carry out the order of your Guru Maharaja. That order is to distribute this mercy to others in the form of your books *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is.*

So it is our constant anxiety, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that when will these fallen souls give up this cycle of birth and death and simply hear your messages of love of Godhead, as we have done, and become ecstatic? The sufferings of the conditioned souls are due to their being neglectful of the order of the Supreme Lord. Therefore great souls and sages have concluded after careful study, that the problems of life, especially in Kali-yuga, can be solved by the methods of widespread propaganda to promote the cult of *bhakti-yoga,* or devotional service to Kṛṣṇa.

So, we, as your disciples, do not know any other shelter than your lotus feet which you so kindly placed upon our heads, in the form of your transcendental books. Therefore, we hold these books very close to our hearts, along with your order of “distribute these books, distribute these books, distribute these books,” because by their distribution the unfortunate inhabitants of Kali-yuga will be delivered.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have saved us from the cycle of repeated birth and death. If somehow or other, by your causeless mercy, we could just keep distributing your books and remember with appreciation your glorious pastimes, our lives will be ecstatic and completely successful.

*Your fallen servants at New Badarīnārāyana*

## Detroit

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

I am the lowest person, and the charge of composing a VYĀSA PŪJĀ homage for your glorification is most taxing for a brain that has been wasted by overindulgence in sense gratification. Some person should be chosen to write this most sacred homage who, with a heart full of love, can go on singing your glories endlessly with full ecstasy and realization. In this, my attempt, I can only think of my shamelessness in daring to even hold a pen with a view toward transcendental glorification of you.

On the other hand, I have seen your real devotees, who are reflections of you, and they are wonderful. I love to see your true followers. They are gorgeous like you. They make me feel small, as I would near you. They show me real happiness as you do. In summary, they remind me of you. Such devotees only, who are rays of your splendor, can properly write, speak, sing and live for your glory. The attempt of one who loves you never fails. In all the world, I admire only those men who have given up the world to love you. They are the greatest, your disciples. I pray to you that I may attain their service life after life for I know that they are sure to always be with you.

At times, I remember your personal *darśanas.* Those were the most wonderful moments of my life. No one but you has ever moved me more deeply, and unless someone is connected to you by service, they can hardly move me at all. After hearing and seeing you, no mundaner is very impressive. You are my favorite singer. I cannot tolerate another author unless he is a Vaisnava. The music you play with your own hands is the most pure and sublime. Your voice is so transcendentally unique that it is not to be imitated. Your qualities and characteristics are to be acquired, and your life is the embodiment of your teachings. I wish that someday I may taste even a small portion of *prasādam* that has been personally cooked by you.

Somehow or other may today be the day that I break through the wall of my selfishness and attain the deepest love for you. Anyone who knows you will try to love you. Those who love you completely are fully happy. You are to be known through your books, and by serving those instructions one will gradually develop full love for you. Therefore anyone who loves you, and feels compassion for those who do not know you, feels obligated to introduce you widely to the world by distribution of your books. AS long as there is one person left in this miserable world that loves you, your book distribution program will go on. It cannot be stopped. This is how love for you is demonstrated.

Although my love for you is so pitiably small, by great fortune I desire to increase it. Let me therefore, by your mercy, always engage in the service of your servants who are so determined to push forward your mission. In that way I shall be able to love you more and more, and I am convinced that by the inconceivable arrangements of Lord Kṛṣṇa the day will come when I shall be able to please you.

On behalf of all the devotees at Śrī Devasadana Mandira, I humbly request your blessings.

## Durban

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Once again we are celebrating the anniversary of your most glorious appearance on the face of the planet, and once again words fail us when we try to express our appreciation of your sublime activities and our gratitude for your incomparable mercy in delivering wretches like ourselves from the crushing embrace of *māyā.* The past year has been noteworthy for a number of reasons, and, without a doubt, one of the most significant of these has been the publishing of the first two volumes of your amazing biography. As your disciples, we had frequently heard tales of your struggles in India prior to your coming to the West, and in the early days in America before the time your movement really took root in the hearts of the young Westerners and began developing into the institution we see today.

However, very few of us has a clear idea of exactly what you went through in those long years, and of the unswerving dedication with which you met and overcame what would have been insurmountable obstacles for one who was not a cent percent pure devotee of the Lord.

After all these years we are now so fortunate in that all these previously only vaguely known activities of yours have been revealed to us, and we feel that this has significantly assisted us in appreciating you and has deepened our attachment for you. Your tireless efforts to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness at even the slightest opportunity are the perfect example for such faltering, neophyte devotees as ourselves. You were literally risking life and limb without a second thought—all to execute the instruction of your beloved Guru Mahārāja—and this impeccable demonstration of transcendental determination makes an indelible mark on our minds. We must follow in your footsteps, spreading the message of the Supreme Lord to the conditioned souls.

We pray that you will give us the transcendental vision and guidance to always remain fixed in your mission and to never be deviated from the service of your lotus feet. Please help us to remain spiritually enthused—ever-increasingly so—to glorify Kṛṣṇa and to penetrate all levels of this Kali-yuga materialistic civilization with forceful preaching and the *hari-nāma.*

Let your personal example stimulate us to the point that we will sacrifice our prides, opinions, and desires for your cause. If we can do that, the world can surely be saved from its hellish future, and, someday, perhaps you will feel a little pleased with us.

*Your servants at ISKCON Durban,*

## East Lansing

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. We can read in your biography that although it was very much of a burden, you patiently tolerated all sorts of obstacles in order to usher in this world-saving Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. With my limited vision, I can immediately see two major reasons for this. One is the instruction of your Guru Maharaja, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvati Gosvami. From the moment that you accepted your revered spiritual master, you took his instructions as your life and soul. Your life became centered around his divine instructions. Your desire was so strong to bring the pure message of loving devotion to Lord Kṛṣṇa to the West that you would walk miles underneath the hot sun just to save a penny or two which could be used to print some Kṛṣṇa consciousness materials in the English language. You are the perfect *aśārya* because your life was a pure example of how the disciple should surrender his every breath for carrying out the instructions of his spiritual master, which is the essence of spiritual life. Once in 1935, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta told you, “If you ever get money, print books.” The rest of your life was decorated by that instruction, which is proving to be the saving grace for the whole human society. So far under your direction, over one hundred million of these gems have been distributed throughout the world. Therefore our humble prayer unto your lotus feet, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is this: Please be kind upon us, who are so weak and fallen. Our entire lives have been based on becoming expert in shirking responsibility and avoiding authority. Now, in this most important hour, please allow us to shed these low-class Kali-yuga mentalities and become responsible devotees underneath your direction. Please make us sold out to your divine instructions as you were ever so loyal to those messages of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta. Please make us aware that your instructions to us are the priceless lifeline to Lord Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet.

The second reason that I perceive for your descent on these Western shores, is your all-encompassing compassion on the fallen, conditioned souls. You left the sublime, transcendental abode of Lord Kṛṣṇa in Vṛndāvana and came to the dark, cold abode of winos and derelicts in the midst of America’s hedonistic capital, New York City. There was no one to meet you when you landed on the dock. No band playing, no newspaper reporters, no television cameras announcing to America that our eternal guide and savior had arrived. Only cold asphalt streets carrying lonely broken hearts being continually beaten by *māyā’s* cracking whip. You searched for spiritual candidates amongst our beaten selves but found only two-legged animals being pushed forward by time to our inevitable slaughter. Yet, you were not one to be discouraged. You knew that Lord Kṛṣṇa’s desire was to bring our fallen selves back to our eternal service to Him. And you also understood that you were the receptacle which was to be used to pour the nectar of the holy name upon our burning, materialistic heads and save us from our parched taste for *māyā.* Like a brave hero who rushes fearlessly into a burning building to save the endangered people, your bold preaching pierced through our mundane conceptions and saved us from our previously inevitable demise. Therefore, Śrīla Prabhupāda, our lives belong to you. For without the presence of your lotus feet, we would have never been able to understand what real life is—-eternal life. We would have never been able to understand what true activity is—-loving, devotional service to Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. We would have never been able to understand what our real identity is—-transcendental to these temporary coverings. And we would have never been able to understand what it is to have a relationship with a truly loving father—-your divine self, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

On this most auspicious of all days, the day that you came to save this world from the thick thundering clouds of ignorance, we again surrender our lives unto you. Please qualify us to become your eternal sons. Please purify our hearts so that we can carry on your mission in cooperation, making our love for you our common bind. Above all, Śrīla Prabhupāda, please continue to guide us closer and closer to our eternal relationship with your loving lotus feet.

*Your indebted sons and daughters in East Lansing,*

## France

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our prostrated *daṇḍavats* and this humble prayer in the dust of your lotus feet.

O blessed event be remembered forever appearance of Prabhupada who came to deliver Gauranga’s message to the entire world to Kali’s disciples wretched and spoiled.  
O most blessed event inspire our hearts appearance of Prabhupada who trained up guards to protect the innocent from Kali’s spell by distributing books in heaven and hell.  
O most blessed event more precious than gold appearance of Prabhupada who did unfold the secret of chanting of the holy name the only hope in Kali’s cruel game.  
O most blessed event be remembered forever appearance of Prabhupada the ever well-wisher of all living beings fallen and hopeless giving them life in this world of death.  
O most blessed event inspire our hearts appearance of Prabhupada whose words like darts pierced the shell of our false ego armor touching our souls revealing their glamour.  
O most blessed event more precious than gold appearance of Prabhupada who determined and bold gave out the mercy of Nitāi-Gaur without discrimination to the rich and poor.  
O most blessed event be remembered forever appearance of Prabhupada who descended to gather Jagāis and Mādhāis from rock concert stages and transform them with love into chanting sages.  
O most blessed event inspire our hearts appearance of Prabhupada who with Jagannatha carts theater, film and diorama spread the glory of Kṛṣṇa and Rama.  
O most blessed event more precious than gold appearance of Prabhupada who never grew old young and fresh in his spiritual form a Vaikuntha man undying –unborn.  
O most blessed event be remembered forever appearance of Prabhupada who descended to sever the knot in the heart of his faithful devotee and bring him to Kṛṣṇa to be happy and free.  
O most blessed event inspire our hearts appearance of Prabhupada who never departs from those who keep him as their life and soul in the form of his vāṇī perfect and whole.  
O most blessed event more precious than gold appearance of Prabhupada who once us told that one moon alone can light up the sky and dispel all darkness; O brothers, let’s try to become all full moons not one but millions to spread Kṛṣṇa’s glories throughout the three worlds.

*Your most unworthy servants from ISKCON France,*

## Gainesville

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, savior and saint,  
Deliverer of Gaurāṅga’s message without restraint.  
Determined to preach and rescue all,  
Saving everyone from māyā’s call.  
If not for you and Your Divine Grace,  
Then all we would see is Kali-yuga’s face.  
But because of your mercy and your loving glance,  
Then is some reason to sing and to dance.  
Lost we all were, devoted to sin,  
Unable to follow that voice from within.  
But then you came, holding your torch.  
Leading us all to Govinda’s front porch.  
Without your mercy I can’t bear to dwell  
On where we should be—in what kind of hell.  
Kali seems horrible—-a big roaring beast,  
But by your grace it has diminished at least.  
We may think it looks bad with all the vie and the sin,  
But without your advent, try multiplying by ten,  
You’ve left us here to continue your fight  
Wrestling with Kali and all of his might.  
You’ve gone back to Kṛṣṇa—-I know this is true,  
And sometimes I wonder, “Now what to do?”  
But then I think, “There’s no time to space out,”  
Serving you is what life is all about.  
Preaching to everyone, not missing a soul,  
Carrying Kṛṣṇa’s message to minimize Kali’s toll.  
We thank you again for your kind loving grace,  
Always thinking of you while we serve you in this place.

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your lotus feet. Your pastimes are amazing. From a material point of view, you came from a backwards, poverty-stricken country to a thriving, progressive country; and as an elderly gentleman with very little money and no friends, you came to America, won the dedication of thousands of persons from every social position, and started an international movement based on the sale of religious literature and repeated chanting of the names of God, in a foreign language, at a time when atheism was on the rise.

If even the greatest doubter approached you a little submissively, you would immediately win him over with your abundant knowledge, faith, humility, compassion, honesty, enthusiasm, wit, resolute determination, youthful vigor, and an unlimited list of saintly qualities that you possessed.

You were sometimes like a very protective mother protecting your childlike disciples from the falls and bruises of this material world. Sometimes you were like a father trying to encourage growth in his offspring by forcing them to take responsibility. And sometimes you were childlike in your straightforwardness and the way you automatically spawned a desire from everyone to render some service to you.

Your composure and confidence were so great that anyone could immediately understand that you were an aristocrat.

You were a world traveler, known and respected by many famous people of the world, but you were always kind, concerned, and helpful to those souls who were completely dependent on your mercy.

There is no greater personality than you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and all we hanker for is the blessing of eternal remembrance of you and eternal service to your lotus feet.

*Your eternal servants, the devotees of Alachua farm and Gainsville preaching center,*

## Gauhati

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

On this auspicious occasion you have given us another chance to remember you, which is the perfection of our Kṛṣṇa consciousness. So much of your personal sacrifice has been reveled to us after you have left this planet, and this has left me lamenting why I did not relieve you more sincerely.

Actually the more your pastimes are discussed by your disciples, the more I understand how you alone have done everything, and are still doing everything now.

Many people thought that after your departure ISKCON would not be able to expand its preaching as when you were here personally. But they have failed to understand Your Divine Grace and ISKCON are non-different, and your transcendental hand is still here guiding our every movement.

One day on a morning walk in South India, I mentioned to you how another Gaudiya Vaisnava society had tried to criticize by saying, “If Caitanya Mahāprabhu wanted Kṛṣṇa consciousness spread in the Western countries, then why didn’t He go there Himself and preach?” When you heard that you stopped abruptly and put your cane down and said, “Caitanya Mahāprabhu did not go because He wanted that I should go and take the credit.” All of the devotees liked to hear this very much, and this is why on this most auspicious occasion we are still giving you all of the credit for everything you have ever done and what you are still doing by your inconceivable mercy.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please always let me be in constant association with you by following your instructions and give me the strength to carry out your desire to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

*Begging to remain your eternal servants,*

Bhavabhūti dāsa / Pradumnya dāsa / Vaikuṇṭha dāsa / Kishenlal / Dvija Govinda dāsa / Nārada Muni dāsa / Hari Paṇḍita dāsa / Maṅgalā Gopī

**League of Devotees**

Uday Shanker Kalita / Ram Sharma / Prof. Jagdish Choudhary / Mrs. J. Choudhary / Dr. Chakraborty and family / Mrs. Ma Saha / Prabir Ram Barooah

## Germany

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Five hundred years ago Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu, who is Himself the Supreme Personality of Godhead, descended from the spiritual world with His intimate associates to inaugurate the *yuga-dharma* of *hari-nāma-saṅkīrtana* in order to deliver the fallen souls of the age of Kali. At the time of His descent the civilized lands of the earth were populated by hardhearted materialists and pseudo spiritualists puffed up with the false pride of self-righteous, no devotional systems of *dharma, Aretha, kāma* and *mockṣa*. But Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu displayed in His ecstatic *līlā* the ultimate goal of human progress: pure love of Kṛṣṇa is awakened by the simple chanting of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mashā mantra.* In this revolutionary manner Lord Caitanya upset the course of history, shattering the disdainful pride of all sorts of no devotees, and in fact delivered the whole universe of living beings who were manifested as conscious embodied species. However, the universe was immediately filled up again by unmanifested beings who were promoted to conscious existence by the Lord’s causeless mercy.

Upon the Lord’s departure, His movement seemed to become obscured as the new occupants of this universal sphere attempted to carry on the arrogant Kali-yuga culture of their predecessors. His intimate disciples, most notably the six Gosvāmīs of Vṛndāvana, had compiled books revealing the complete science of Kṛṣṇa consciousness as propounded by Lord Caitanya, but as time went on it seemed that these teachings of the Lord could not be spread with the vigor that they so urgently deserved. Materialism and impersonalism again become rampant.

But yet Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya’s plan for the deliverance of all fallen souls in the Kali age was not to be daunted. By the arrangement of the Lord’s external energy the destructive effects of Māyāvāda philosophy degenerated into Māyāvāda insanity, and the intelligence of the human race become more and more covered by bewilderment and illusion. As civilization became collectively more dull-headed and depraved, its demoniac, self-assured determination born of puffed-up pride began to wilt in the face of impeding disaster.

As introspective wise men in the West began to rebel against the predominant philosophies of their time, in India Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu began to insert the pin of Kṛṣṇa consciousness into the fabric of human thinking. Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Thākura, an intimate servant of the Lord, appeared to reestablish the pure teachings of Mahāprabhu, which seemed to be lost. And most significantly, in the year 1896 he sent an English work of his own composition, *Śrī Caitanya –His Life and Precepts,* to the McGill University in Canada.

Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvati Thākura, the son of Bhaktivinoda Thākura, and disciple of Gaura-kiśora dāsaBābājī, later created an active preaching movement based upon the life and precepts of Lord Caitanya, which in a few short years made its influence felt all over the Indian sub-continent. He expressed a strong desire to export his movement to the Western world, as his father had exported his book.

In the same year that Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Thākura sent his book to Canada, His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupada appeared in this world to fulfill the desire of his predecessor-*ācāryas.* He later became an initiated disciple of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvati Thākura, and imbibed from him the practical application of the philosophy of Lord Caitanya, which he himself had learned in principle from birth. At a time when the confusion wrought by impersonalism and atheism (*nirviśeṣa* and śūnyavādi) had reached an all-time high in the West, Śrīla Prabhupāda broke through the world’s intellectual smoke screen of conflicting doctrines like the plow of Lord Balarama (Lord Nityananda , the original spiritual master) to reveal the eternal, transcendental message of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Behind him he is dragging the entire human race, who are being forced to dance in ecstasy like helpless puppets, all the way back home back to Godhead.

The *Śrī Caitanya-cariṭamṛta* does not reveal events of some distant past which have forever faded into posterity. Indeed, in this book, so ably translated and purported in English by Śrīla Prabhupāda, the eternal future is revealed for all those who read it or hear it or touch it or see it or even think about it once. That future will be visible within the material world for the next ten thousand years, and visible forever within the hearts of those who are swept away by Śrīla Prabhupāda’s mercy. That future, which Bhaktivinoda Thākura longed to see, has been revealed at last by you, my spiritual master—Śrīla Prabhupāda.

It is not enough for me to delve into my vocabulary for superlative adjectives which only rightfully describe a tiny fraction of your glories. You will not sit idly awaiting my inept, fumbling preparations of garlands of words—-you have too much to do. Of course, you are so kind upon us that you allow us to offer you praise in this day, but what is the meaning of these flowery words if I am unable to assist you in your great mission, the mission of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu, showing no tangible accomplishment of his desires for preaching, I have no one to chastise except my own ridiculous, posturing self. Remembering your splendid qualities, I can only lament for how I have wasted my life until now.

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, I have followed the will-o-the wisp of sense enjoyment and mental speculation into the swamp of this material world, and now I am hopelessly lost. The path I tried leads only further into murky darkness. There are dangerous crocodiles of atheism awaiting underfoot to gobble me up as well as all sorts of other fearful creatures which I can hear screaming and chattering in the distance. And yet you are so kind to me, even though I am in this hellish situation of my own doing, for you have mercifully extended a ray of light my way by which I may find my way to your lotus feet. I can only hope to repay you for your kindness in some unimaginably distant birth, as I am so foolish, so unqualified in my present conditioned state. I can make no claim of service to you, I can make no claim of understanding your position, I can make no claim of even a clear-headed appreciation of your mercy upon me. And yet I am not lost to you, nor are you lost to me. Falling at your lotus feet again and again, I pray for your continued mercy. Please don’t kick me away.

*The German ISKCON Yātrās:*

Berlin / Heidelberg / Munich / New Jiyaḍa Nṛsiṁha-kṣetra Farm

## Gītā-nāgarī

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

**The history**

Shortly after the assassination of Mahatma Gandhi, around 1948 or 1949, Śrīla Prabhupāda wrote an essay titled *Interpretation of the Bhagavad-gītā.* During this period there were funds being collected for a memorial to honor Gandhi, and there many opinions as to what the best use of the funds would be. Śrīla Prabhupāda seized upon this situation, as always, to present pure Kṛṣṇa consciousness. He identified Gandhi with the teachings of the *Bhagavad-gītā* and said, “If you really want to honor Gandhi don’t make some big material arrangement, a hospital or a big statue of him, but spread the teachings of *Bhagavad-gītā* which he stood for.” He then began his essay using the words Gītānāgarī in the broadest sense, almost interchangeably with Kṛṣṇa consciousness, saying that this concept of Gītā-nāgarī has to be introduced all over the world. Then, however, Śrīla Prabhupāda began almost mysteriously, to describe in detail a specific village, with a very special mission, Gītā-nāgarī, the place where the *Bhagavad-gītā* is always sung.

**The prophecy**

“From Gītā-nagari, this universal truth must be propagated systematically so that real human society may be reestablished for the benefit of all, dividing men according to natural mode by such approved processes as are called ‘practical psychology’ or anything else. The Indians must set example to open hospitals to cure diseases of the heart and different stages of the mode of treatment shall be conducted in the Gītā-nagari, according to the plan of Bhagavad-gītā.” Then he has underlined and typed in capitals the following sentence. “IT SHALL BE THE FOREMOST BUSINESS OF GĪTĀ-NAGARI TO CHALK OUT ITS PLAN OF ACTION STRICTLY ON THE TEACHINGS OF BHAGAVAD-GĪTĀ AND NOTHING MORE AND NOTHING LESS.”

“It the inhabitants of Gītā-nagari want to establish perfect Kingdom of Heaven, they can endeavor to do so combinedly in the service of the Personality of Godhead. The inhabitants of Gītā-nagari shall, therefore, engage their everything; namely, their life, wealth, intelligence, and their words, in the service of the Lord. That is the practical way of living a life of Bhagavad-gītā.

“The Gītā-nagari shall be the center for supreme culture of knowledge. Gītā-nagari shall set example that neither God or the living being nor nature is, in any way, antagonistic to one another, but all of them exist in harmony as a complete unit. When Gītā-nagari will attempt to harmonize such sweet relation between men and God, man to world, and world to God, at such an auspicious time only the United Nations’ effort will be successful.” Then again in capital letters and underlined: “THE MAIN FUNCTION OF ŚRĪ GĪTĀ-NAGARI SHALL BE TO INCREASE THE NUMBER OF DEVOTEES ONLY.”

As Śrīla Prabhupāda continues his divine instructions he becomes more and more specific. Next he describes our Good Life Company, which at present distributes *prasādam* all over the world. “There are so many asuras and if you want to do the asuras some good, it is our duty to distribute the remnants of foodstuff offered to Godhead to the asuras so that they can rise up gradually to the transcendental plane. The Gītā-nagari has to arrange for such distribution of prasadam in order to feed the hungry asuras. The asuras are always hungry and distressed and if arrangement for distributing prasadam is made for them, nobody will object to accept such prasadam.”

Then Śrīla Prabhupāda described the kind of management he desired, not only for the specific Gītā-nagari but for the worldwide of ISKCON Society. “In order to give effect to the program of the Gītā-nagari, it is necessary that at least twelve gentlemen……should form an association and, with the help of these gentlemen, arrangement has to be done to provide the fund of Gītā-nagari…..The Gītā-nagari, therefore, will be able to set a noble example for the benefit of the world as it will show how wealth can be engaged in the service of the internal potency of Godhead and, thereby, bring peace and prosperity on the world. Such twelve gentlemen, when they have assembled in an organized form, shall chalk out the program of the Gītā-nagari. And to guide the principles of such activities, Bhagavad-gītā will be the book of knowledge or medium of information. It is incumbent that such governing bodies of the Gītā-nagari must follow the preliminary qualifications, namely they must not have illegitimate connection with women, they must be completely vegetarian, they must refrain themselves from all sorts of intoxicating habits, they must refrain from all sorts of gambling.” Amazing!!!

Further, Śrīla Prabhupāda specifically stated, “The proprietor and Deity of the Gītā-nagari will be Śrī Krishna Himself. The temple of Śrī Krishna, which will be situated in the center of the Gītā-nagari, shall contain the vigraha of Śrī Krishna placed on the chariot of Śrī Arjuna seated along with Śrī Krishna. The vigraha shall be known as Partha-sarathi. The murti of Śrī Partha-sarathi shall proclaim throughout the world as how much the personality of Godhead can be merciful to His devotee, as one of the many examples of how the Supreme God is friend to friend.”

The essay continues by describing in more and more detail specific duties of the members, including *varṇāśrama* engagements, *guru-kula,* different preaching activities and even different kinds of constructions like a temple, schools, etc.

**The visit**

In the summer of 1976 Śrīla Prabhupāda finally came to visit his Gītā-nagari, already a practical expansion of his earlier plans. During his arrival address he once again revealed the future plans of Gītā-nagari. “Kṛṣṇa, in His natural life, is a village boy in Vṛndāvana…..There should be Kṛṣṇa consciousness everywhere, but if we remain in a properly organized village, as Kṛṣṇa has personally shown us by example, then that is very good…..The Supreme Controller is giving us instructions, and if we follow these simple instructions——to remain in this village and be satisfied with this simple life, subsisting on agricultural produce and milk—-then where is the difficulty? There is no economic problem……Live in this village and be satisfied with the bare necessities of life. There is no need of luxury, save time and chant Hare Kṛṣṇa. This is ideal living.

“I am very pleased that you are doing this. Do it very nicely so that others may be attracted. This industrial factory life is asuric life. It is killing the human ambition. It is killing civilization. There are many occupations for a Kṛṣṇa conscious person, because in the *Bhagavat-gītā* Kṛṣṇa speaks of sociology, politics, philosophy, religion, culture, and economics. Everything is there if you simply try to follow Kṛṣṇa’s instruction.” Amazing—-Śrīla Prabhupāda at Gītā-nagari farm in Pennsylvania repeating the same message as in the 1949 essay!

**The prayer**

Our love for you, our spiritual master, can only be expressed by our enthusiastic willingness to abandon all of our perverted desires and to replace them with your pure and wonderful dream, Śrīla Prabhupāda. If, somehow or other, we the residents of your Gītā-nagari can learn to cooperate with your master plan, then and only then can we be your true servants. We pray at your lotus feet, Śrīla Prabhupāda, please, please, please help us to engage everything: our lives, wealth, intelligence, and words, in your eternal service.

*Your servants at Gītā-nagari,*

## Glasgow

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
The most exalted transcendental hero.  
To you, Gurudeva, I bow this heavy head.  
Heavy with pride, heavy with doubt  
And heavy with tears of love unshed.

O my master, a traitor now begs your pardon  
“Souled-out” to māyā, my empty heart did harden.

Alas, this mire of delusion is so deep  
Must catch hold of your lotus feet!

Hoping against hope to get on your boat  
O captain supreme, only way to stay afloat

Right or wrong you’re always right  
Please save me from this fearful plight.

The task of saving the wild, wild West  
Verily required Lord Kṛṣṇa’s very best.

You are the messiah  
Sent by your Guru Maharaja  
To the desert animals  
For destroying their mirage.

Your mission carries on  
With more and more vigor  
As your sons, daughters and grand-ones  
Make it bigger and bigger and bigger!

Kalki will not see your ISKCON folk  
They’ll all be back home in Kṛṣṇaloka.

Continuing here without you,  
Is a fate far worse than death,  
The only shelter left  
Your instructions to our chests!

Your books illumine standards for us all  
We’d better read them to avert a sudden fall!

My name is dāsa  
The servant of the Lord.  
Kindly fulfill this meaning  
O father most adored.

All glories, all glories, a trillion times and on  
To Prabhupada, founder-aśārya of ISKCON!

Dānavīra dāsaGaur-vanacārī / Sārvabhauma Bhaṭṭācārya dāsa brahmacari / Jim Buchanan / David Porter / Billy Lauchlin

## Graz

*Anuvratānāṁ śiṣyānāṁ*  
*Putrāṇāṁ ca dvijittama*  
*Anāpṛṣṭam api brūyur*  
*Guravo dīna-vatsalāḥ*

**“O best among** ***brāhmaṇas,*** **those who are spiritual masters are very kind to the needy. They are always kind to their followers, disciples, and sons, and without being asked by them, they describe all that is knowledge.” (*****Bhāg.*** **3.7.36)**

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept my humble obeisances at your glowing lotus feet, my only and eternal shelter. It is a fact that you went to America simply on the order of your spiritual master and—-as the *śloka* above explains—-because you are so kind and compassionate you could not (and still cannot) tolerate the sufferings of the illusioned living beings. Although they are not really interested (especially if they hear that this process will cleanse them from all the nice dirty desires and inclinations for material sense enjoyment), still with great strength you shake their shoulders, shouting “*Jīva jago!* Wake up! Chant Hare Kṛṣṇa! Come on, serve Kṛṣṇa!” This is your only concern: to lift up and save the unfortunate, suffering beings like myself deeply sunk in the ocean of nescience. You only think of how to help others. Who can understand this kindness of yours? All our suffering and misfortune is due to ignorance about our spiritual nature. You dispel this nescience, thus by your help alone will we be able to reestablish our loving relationship with Śrī Kṛṣṇa. By so doing you are rendering the greatest, in fact the only real service to humanity. All the so-called leaders, humanitarians, and politicians are like the masses, blind spiritually; thus they misguide the people in general, leading them to a dark, hellish future. As one guest to a Sunday feast has remarked: “What is very unique in this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is, that although today everyone talks of progress and marching *forward,* instead they just degrade themselves. On the other hand, here is Prabhupada who says, ‘Let’s go *back* home, *back* to Godhead.” And actually this is the real progress! To progress back to our original Kṛṣṇa consciousness, that which we once had but now have forgotten. Only a pure devotee like you, coming directly from the spiritual world with the mission to save us fallen souls, can guide us. You were never illusions by *māyā!* Empowered by Kṛṣṇa, you are the expert captain of the ship of Kṛṣṇa consciousness on the ocean of material existence. First you pull us out of the troubled water of ignorance and suffering, then you guide the ship safely to the other side of the ocean, back to the spiritual world. You are prepared—-exactly like an ideal captain—-to leave last, letting everyone else first land in the spiritual world. You are indeed the embodiment of Kṛṣṇa’s compassion and mercy. (As it is said that by the mercy of Kṛṣṇa one gets a *guru,* and by the mercy of that *guru* he will get Kṛṣṇa.)

Once I foolishly asked you about how I could repay you. After studying your books more carefully I came to understand that I am but a *vinoda-pātram* or joker, because if anyone tries to repay the spiritual master he will simply become a laughingstock just like a joker, since it is *not possible* to repay such a debt. All we can and have to do is to follow your instructions; and by embracing your mission, dedicate ourselves eternally in your service. In other words, we simply have to become tools in your hands. You want us to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness through the world. This is your mission. This should not be very difficult if we just act as you taught. I mean to say we do not have to invent or concoct anything new. We simply have to learn your way of doing things, how to do Prabhupada preaching, Prabhupada *kīrtana,* Prabhupada distribution of books and *prasādam,* Prabhupada managing, Prabhupada cooking, etc. If we acquire thoroughly your system, the Prabhupada system in doing everything, then the success will be guaranteed. You were training us with so much care, and you wanted us to learn all these. This does not mean cheap imitation but, as recommended in the *śāstras,* following sincerely in the footsteps of the *ācāryas.* We can never be independent of your help and mercy. This is all that holds us, and if we forget this, we will certainly fall down, like a person cutting the same branch of a tree on which he sits. But again, all I did in this material world was cheating, sinning, and forgetting. How then will I succeed in keeping honestly to your instructions in my heart without forgetting your mercy, thus cheating myself again ( by surrendering to *māyā’s* illusory promises)? Therefore, my dear Gurudeva, kindly give me strength, give me help, and drag or push my crawling self along the path leading back to Kṛṣṇa! Kindly crush my stone heart so that I will be attracted to the chanting of the holy name! Please allow me to take the dust of your lotus feet and keep it on my head! Falling down repeatedly before you like a stick, I ask you to keep me in your service eternally.

*Arthābhāvaṁ viniścitya*  
*Pratītasyāpi nātmanaḥ*  
*tāṁ cāpi yuṣmac-caraṇa-*  
*sevayāham prarāṇude*

**“My dear sir, I am obliged to you because now I can understand that this material manifestation is without substance, although it appears real. I am confident that by serving your feet it will be possible for me to give up the false idea.” (*****Bhāg.*** **3.7.18)**

*Your sons and grandsons in Graz, Austria,*

Dvārakeśa dāsa brahmacari / Paramajyotī dāsa brahmacari / Vaidyanātha dāsa brahmacari / Abhaya caraṇa dāsa brahmacari / Īśvaracandra dāsa adhikārī / Tulasī-bhakti-devī dāsī

## Guadalajara

Since time immemorial I had been looking for something without even knowing what I really wanted. However due to a great fortune I have found you, and you have bestowed your causeless mercy upon me, a fallen soul, by telling me that Kṛṣṇa is all that I need, giving me His wonderful holy name, that enchanting melody of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mantra.* It is because of this compassionate gesture to such a lowly and fallen person that I am indebted to you. How can it be possible for me to repay or even to appreciate the causeless mercy of the all-merciful one, the Supreme Personality of Godhead?

Of all kinds of worship, the worship of God is the most important; and of all types of worship of God, the worship of Lord Kṛṣṇa is the best; and of all types of worship of Kṛṣṇa, the worship of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu is the most sublime method of self-realization. And the worship of His worshiper is even more auspicious, as stated by the Lord Himself, *mad-bhakta-pūjāvyādhikā:* “Better than the worship of Me is the worship of My devotee.” Among all the devotees of Lord Caitanya, your divine self is the most glorious because you are directly carrying out Lord Caitanya’s mission, and therefore satisfying His desire.

*pṛthivīte āche yata nagarādi grāma*  
*sarvatra pracāra haibe mora nāma*  
*\[CB Antya-khaṇḍa 4.126\]*

**“The holy name will be spread through every town and village.”**

Śrī Nityānanda Rāma, the original spiritual master, was very merciful to Jagāi and Mādhāi. Therefore, the glorious activities of the ambassador representative of Kṛṣṇa are above my capacity of appreciation. As stated in the *śāstra, vaiṣṇavera kriyā-mudrā vijñe na bhujhāya:* “No one can understand the activities of a pure devotee.”

Even though Śeṣa-nāga has been singing about and glorifying the transcendental qualities and attributes of Lord Kṛṣṇa with His unlimited hoods since time immemorial, he cannot find the limit to this ocean of transcendental qualities of the Lord. In a similar way, you manifest this same nature. So I beg for your shelter because I am feeling incapable to protect myself and fearful that *māyā* might come and trap me in her nets; and because I have no other shelter in this world than you, I bow down again and again asking for your help. Please let me always be with you! Please don’t kick me away!

*Your unworthy servants of Gaudalajara Mandira,*

## Guyana

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. We have all been tasting material life since time immemorial and were dragged here and there by *māyā* and engaging in every sinful activity. It was only when your transcendental influence began to spread through the globe that we were saved from total (and probably eternal) degradation. Because we were living the life of animals engaged in the four pillars of sinful life, we were unable to hear you, touch or see you—-for associating with you means having a taste of Vaikuṇṭha-dhāma.

You are the most munificent because you did not discriminate in giving the holy name. With the weapon of the holy name you are converting us—-*mlecchas* and *yavanas—*-into devotees. By your transcendental arrangement we are able to meet your pure representatives who are pushing forward your mission. Through the association of Ādi Keśava Swami and Satsvarūpa Goswami we are relishing that nectar that you are giving to your disciples, granddisciples and generations of disciples to come. We are realizing what Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Thākura meant when he sang:

*śuddha-bhakata-caraṇa-reṇu*  
*bhajana-anukūla*  
*bhakata-sevā parama-siddhi*  
*prema-latikāra-mūla*

**“Dust from the lotus feet of pure devotees is conducive to devotional service, while service to the Vaisnavas is itself the supreme perfection and the root of the tender creeper of divine love.”**

Sometimes we wonder what we would have been now if we had not received your message—-certainly we would have been rotting in this filth of material existence.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are eternally indebted to you. Please shower your mercy on us and give us the knowledge and strength to push on the cult of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu with the help of your disciple Satsvarūpa Goswami.

*Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda.

**ISKCON Guyana Uitvlugt center**

## Harmony Farm (Oklahoma)

In a house on the Gaṅgā  
On this glorious day,  
Of Nandotsava  
Appeared Abhay Charan De.

With kartāl and drum  
Sing the holy name,  
Herald the day  
Our Prabhupada came.

With flags and festoons  
Decorate your door,  
Every home a temple  
Of Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaura!

Collect the cows and bulls  
Prepare sumptuous feasts,  
Erect triumphant arches  
From the West to East.

With Prabhupada on a palanquin  
Upon a royal seat,  
In a grand procession  
Dance through every street.

Come with satin slippers  
And elegant rings,  
Superexcellent presents  
Just fit for a king.

Sew new silks, bring sandalwood  
And a stately carved cane,  
Publish some marvelous books  
To increase your fame.

Gather throngs of people  
From all over the earth,  
Make a tumultuous roar  
Glorifying his birth.

To see everyone drowning  
In an ocean of bliss,  
Immersed in your service  
If they only what they miss.

The world is unconscious  
Of the most miraculous day,  
We can’t live without sharing you;  
For that only we pray.

*Begging to remain your servants,*  
The devotees of Harmony Farm,

## Hatford

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānañjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai Śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. As we try to properly glorify you by this offering on this most holy of all days—-the occasion of your appearance in this world—-the words seem too inadequate. So we stop—try again—and ultimately go on only with the understanding that in all of our activities, all we can do is try our best to please you, constantly praying for your mercy and trying to follow in your footsteps. By always keeping your instructions and example in our hearts, by your grace, we can achieve the ultimate goal of life—-love for Kṛṣṇa. As soon as we forget you, our minds again become captured by our enemies in the form of lust, anger, envy, fear, illusion, etc. You have stated regarding your own spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvati Gosvami Maharaja: “I still consider His Divine Grace to be always present with me by his *vāṇī,* his words….I think that His Divine Grace is always seeing my activities and guiding me within my heart by his words….” Śrīla Prabhupāda, how can we always be conscious of you within our hearts—these hearts so long polluted by foul thoughts and desires.”

Before finishing your pastimes in this world you told us: “You can show your love for me when I’m gone by how you cooperate together to push on the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement.” You have also shown us the example of the Pracetās in the *Śrīmad-Bhagavatam* (4.30.8-9): “The Supreme Personality of Godhead said: ‘My dear sons of the King, I am very much pleased by the friendly relationships among you. All of you are engaged in one occupation—-devotional service. I am so pleased by your mutual friendships that I wish you all good fortune….”

Our hope of pleasing you lies in our cooperative effort to serve you in your mission to save all the unfortunate conditioned souls trapped in *māyā’s* tight grip. Here at ISKCON Hartford we are just a handful of devotees, and our temple is relatively new. But we desire to please you very much by spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness throughout Connecticut. We fervently pray at your lotus feet that we may abandon all selfish and independent desires born of false ego and simply work together humbly according to your desire. Please help us to remain conscious of our position as your insignificant servants—giving up all enviousness and duplicitousness in our dealings. And please help us to remember to always chant Hare Kṛṣṇa and thus keep our hearts pure—-fixed in your devotional service.

We beg to remain your humble servants eternally.

*Your disciples and granddisciples at ISKCON Hartford,*

Pyāri Mohana dāsa / Vrajendranandana dāsa / Raktaka dāsa / Pāda-sevana dāsa / Kṛṣṇaloka dāsa

## Hawaiian Islands

**The Captain of Our Ship (Part 11)**

Like fools we have come to this world to be free  
And now we are drowning in the material sea.  
The ocean is rough and the ocean is deep  
But being so foolish we comfortably sleep.  
Not caring for shelter we play in the waves  
Not knowing our future awaits us with graves.  
Enjoying the sleet and enjoying the rain  
Mistaking for pleasure—what is only pain.  
How could we accept this as being our home  
With all our relations like ephemeral foam?  
If we try to cross on our own we must fall  
Like hitching a ride on a little dog’s tail.  
So please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, toss the life ring  
In the form of your mercy we know you did bring.  
Or why would you come to this horrible place  
Except to distribute your unlimited grace?  
Please drag us aboard and take us to port  
Where Kṛṣṇa is loving His eternal consort.  
And always remain as our father and guide  
If even we are just a thorn in your side.  
Please keep us on course and don’t let us slip  
And always remain—captain of our ship.

*Your loving servants in the Hawaiian Islands,*
