## Hong Kong

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our unworthy obeisances at your soft silklike lotus feet. All glories to your ever-increasing desire to elevate the conditioned souls of this material world. I have blasphemed your devotees, disobeyed your instructions, and in general committed most every offense imaginable, but still you are allowing me to take shelter in your ISKCON *saṅkīrtana* movement. Your divine lotus feet provide my only solace in this world of illusion; so let me bow down and worship those lotus feet at every moment.

Śrīla Narottama dāsaThākura sings, *śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya prabhu, dayā koro more / toma vinā ke dayālu jagat-saṁsāre.* He is begging for the mercy of Lord Caitanya because He is the most merciful of all, but we are thinking that you are the actual manifestation of that mercy. But what we cannot understand is how we have been so fortunate as to have your transcendental association. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us real life, eternal life, and now once again we are in the perfect position to work at our eternal life, and now once again we are in the perfect position to work at our eternal engagement, pleasing you. Therefore, we are praying that by the grace of Lord Kṛṣṇa we can catch up the mood which you reflected in your poem written upon your arrival in the U.S.A.

*āniyācho jadi prabhu āmāre nācāte*  
*nācāo nācāo prabhu nācāo se-mate*  
*kāṣṭhera puttai jatha nācāo se-mate*

**“O Lord I am just like a puppet in Your hands. So if You have brought me here to dance, then make me dance, O Lord make me dance as You like.”**

We see your mood as more than total surrender at the lotus feet of Lord Kṛṣṇa. It is the result of that surrender. So now we pray with all humility, you have brought us here to preach, them make us preach, make us preach, make us preach the message of Lord Caitanya as you like.

*We beg to remain your eternal servants,*

## Hopland

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Your Guru Maharaja, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvati, preached for years to establish the mission of Lord Caitanya all over the world. Although his instructions were for all his disciples , you alone have carried his missionary work across the ocean to the fallen souls around the world.

Now, due to your absence, your ISKCON, your teachings, and your missionary spirit have been left in the hands of your most serious disciples who have understood the meaning of your message and are carrying it on in the way you had hoped.

Here at Mt. Kailas only a few of us had the fortune to accept initiation from Your Divine Grace. But as you have stated in the *Caitanya-caritamrta,* if one hears about Kṛṣṇa through Kṛṣṇa conscious literature and through the *paramparā* system of pure Vaisnavas, there is no difficulty in becoming a pure Vaisnava free from mundane desires and personal motivations.

In this way there is hope that what you have given will not be lost, just as what your Guru Maharaja gave you was not lost.

*Eternally indebted to you,*

Your servants at Mt.Kailas,

## Houston

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Tava kathāmṛtaṁ tapta-jīvanaṁ*  
*Kavidhir īḍitaṁ kalmaṣāpaham*  
*śravaṇa-maṅgalaṁ śrīmad-ātataṁ*  
*bhuvi gṛṇanti te bhūridā janāḥ*

**“My Lord, the nectar of Your words and the description of Your activities are the life and soul of those who are always aggrieved in this material world. Those narrations are transmitted by exalted personalities and they eradicate all sinful reaction. Whoever hears these narrations attains all good fortune. These narrations are broadcast all over the world and are filled with spiritual power. Those who spread the message of Godhead are certainly the most munificent welfare workers.” (*****Bhāg.*** **10.31.9)**

Please accept our respectful obeisances at your lotus feet. Out of your causeless mercy, you brought the medicine of *hari-nāma,* and by distributing profusely, you founded and carefully nurtured the ISKCON branch of the Caitanya tree. Now your mission has been expanded and continues to do so more and more every day.

We cannot ever repay our debt to you. We cannot ever fully appreciate the difficulties you underwent to establish Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the world. By reading your *Līlāmṛta* we can have a glimpse of your devotion. By remembering your activities and instructions our faith is solidified, and we become inspired to carry on your mission. We pray that you will bless us with success in our preaching activities.

All glory to you Śrīla Prabhupāda! May your fame be spread all over the three worlds.

*The devotees of ISKCON Houston,*

## Hṛṣīkeśa

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances with a straw in our mouth in the dust of your lotus feet. You have illuminated the whole world with the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Caitanya. We always remember you as the embodiment of all spiritual qualities, as the deliverer of the message of the *Vedas* as it is, as the savior of the fallen souls of the Kalī-yuga, and as Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s mercy personified.

In the duration of your manifested *līlā,* we accepted you as our spiritual master and took shelter under the peaceful umbrella of your International Society for Krishna Consciousness. You instructed us the details when to cut, where to support, how to water, and how to feed the infant creeper of devotion. You gave the hint of the most secret destination, Goloka Vṛndāvana. It is said that one may judge a Vaisnava by how good a touchstone he is, by seeing how many pure and sincere disciples and granddisciples he has made all over the world. We are thinking you are the best of the Vaisnavas, for who else but you has spread this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement everywhere. Men, women, and children of all races and nationality who joined together—-you taught them that your ambition is to see the disciples not only chant, dance, take *prasādam* and follow the regulative principles but also spread the *saṅkirtana* movement everywhere in order to deliver the fallen souls. Of course, it will be ludicrous to compare any other spiritualists with Your Divine Grace.

Only by the mercy of the spiritual master is one able to understand the purpose of the spiritual master, and only by his mercy is one able to fulfill that purpose. Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is clear that because you understood your spiritual master’s desire to soundly defeat the Māyāvāda philosophy on the basis of reason, argument, and *sāstra,* and to establish Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa as the “transcendental autocrat,” he has empowered you to fulfill that desire.

You have already saved so many of us from being swallowed by the black snake of impersonalism. The whole world is being contaminated by the venom of this serpent. Ever from this small, and apparently insignificant city of Hṛṣīkeśa, a poisonous vapor is emanating and causing madness in the minds of millions of innocent, yet ignorant, victims. As Lord Caitanya, *māyāvadī bhāṣya hoya sarva nāśa.* If one hears this Māyāvāda philosophy, his spiritual life is finished. Lord Caitanya, however, being the all-opulent, all-merciful Supreme Personality of Godhead demonstrated that even the Māyāvādīs, whom He considered the greatest offenders, could not escape His loving network in the form of the *saṅkirtana* movement.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your glories are unlimited; even the highest demigods like Śīva, Brahmā, and Candra cannot describe your glories fully. Just as Kṛṣṇa’s glories are unlimited, being His pure devotee your glories are also unlimited. When Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu asked Rāmānanda Rāya, “Who is the most famous person in the world?” Rāmānanda Rāya replied, “A pure devotee of the Lord is the most famous.” Your Divine Grace is not only famous in this insignificant universe, but all over the three worlds. Everyone in the spiritual world must be talking about your glories, about how you have spread this *saṅkirtana* movement all over the world. Therefore, naturally you are the most famous devotee.

When Jesus Christ left, he left twelve disciples behind and his glories are spread all over the world. You left thousands of disciples behind. Your glories will spread even more. Due to lack of disciplic succession, the real message of Christ is lost, but your mission will live forever. Your books and your disciples will never fail you. Your books do not leave any room for misinterpretation. They directly declare the supremacy of the Lord from the beginning to the end. There is no compromise. Everything about is first class. Your books, your lectures, your speaking, your singing, your walking, your talking, your laughing, and cooking, everything is first class. And everyone was always impressed and attached to you just like to Kṛṣṇa. You are not only a pure devotee but you are a *śakty-āveśāvatara* who has changed the course of Kali-yuga. Simply by your advent and preaching, this *yuga* has become worshipable.

We have started to develop this mission here in this *uttara-khaṇḍa,* which is supposed to be very pious and naturally Godly minded. According to Vedic literature this place is very auspicious. Many great personalities such as Lord Rama, His brother Lakṣmaṇa, the five Pāṇḍavas, Vyāsadeva, and Ajāmila came and performed *tapasya* here. Nara-Nārāyaṇa Ṛṣi in the form of Badarīnātha is also here. Dakṣa Prajāpati performed a fire sacrifice here. Lord Brahmā himself built a bathing *ghāṭa* for the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Now this place has turned into the Māyāvādī’s place, being filled with so-called *yogīs, jñānīs, dhyānīs,* and agnostics. We pray for your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to preach this pure Kṛṣṇa consciousness to these people and change them into pure devotees as Lord Caitanya did in Benares. There are millions of people from all over the world who come here to have spiritual life, but they get misguided by so-called spiritualists here. We pray for your mercy to get established here and convince everybody about the actual constitutional activity of the living entity.

*Your eternal, insignificant servants at Hṛṣikśa,*

Kṛṣṇa-Bālarāma dāsa brahmacari / Nāgaraja dāsa brahmacari / Mahānidhi dāsa brahmacari / Gaurarāja dāsa brahmacari / Haripāda dāsa brahmacari / Tripāda-vidhūti dāsa brahmacari / Ambarīśa dāsa brahmacari / Bhakta Devdāsan / Bhakta Sreenivasan / Bhakta Rameshwar

## Ireland

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Today, on the auspicious day of your appearance, let us thank you for your kindness by glorifying your qualities through this poem, taking guidance from the eight verses of Śrīla Viśvanātha Cakravartī Thākura’s Śrī Śrī Gurvaṣṭaka. Mahājano yena gataḥ sa panthāḥ….

**At the Guru’s Lotus Feet I Bow**

The guru is receiving grace  
From ocean’s mercy store.  
Just as a cloud does water  
On a forest fire pour,  
So does the guru save this world  
From flames of gross desire;  
His love, like rain, puts out the pain  
Of lust, which burns like fire.  
There’s none so generous as he,  
A cloud of mercy sweet;  
I bow expecting rainfall  
From his gracious lotus feet.

The guru loves to sing and dance  
In pure ecstatic mood  
And spread Gaurāṅgā’s sankirtana,  
His heart with love imbued;  
And as he twirls and swirls in trance  
And on the name does call,  
His hair stands up, his body shakes  
And tears in torrents fall.  
I pray that we may sing and dance  
At home and on the street  
And bring all songless souls to prance  
Before his lotus feet.

The guru loves to worship  
At the temple of the Lord,  
Where forms of Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa  
Are devotedly adored.  
He leads his dear disciples  
In such service divine  
As dressing up the Deities  
In garments fair and fine,  
And cleaning out the temple.  
Thus one’s life becomes complete  
By guileless surrender  
At the guru’s lotus feet.

The guru offers Kṛṣṇa  
Four delicious kinds of food,  
Analyzed as food that’s sucked,  
Licked, drunk and chewed;  
And when he sees the devotees  
Respect prasādam with glee,  
His heart is glad, for such prasadam  
Fills life with jollity.  
What joy it is to take prasadam!  
Thus, even as we eat,  
Devoid of greed, we can recall  
The guru’s lotus feet.

There’s but one thing  
To which the guru eagerly aspires,  
Of which he hears and talks and sings  
And never ever tires—  
The guru longs to taste, at every step,  
The loving games  
Of Radhika and Madhava,  
Their features, forms and names.  
Such pure and sinless subjects,  
So refreshing to repeat,  
Are relished by the mercy  
Of the guru’s lotus feet.

The guru lends his expert hand  
To aid the love affair  
Of Radhika and Madhava,  
The transcendental pair.  
He helps the maids and sakhīs  
With their confidential plans  
To unite the loving couple  
In the blissful rāsa-dance.  
‘Tis by his grace so many souls  
In harmony can meet,  
Uniting in the holy name  
And service to his feet.

The guru and the Lord are one  
And equal in this way:  
The guru is God’s faithful son  
And says what God would say.  
Thus all the scriptural books confirm  
And all the saints agree  
That God and guru be obeyed  
And worshiped equally.  
The guru, therefore, sits aloft  
Upon the exalted seat  
And we small souls try our best  
To serve his lotus feet.

It is by guru’s grace alone  
That Kṛṣṇa is obtained;  
Without his grace, if he’s displeased,  
No benefit is gained.  
And so we should remember him  
And praise his worthy name,  
And try throughout the universe  
To propagate his fame.  
Three times a day I bow to him  
And humbly do entreat:  
“O Gurudeva, please give me shade  
Beneath your lotus feet!”

*From the devotees of ISKCON Ireland,*

**Dublin**

## Italy

You have now shown all of us that you are guiding this movement through the G.B.C. body. So the duty of the servant is to serve according to the direction of the master, not by his own whim. Therefore we fully surrender to your plan for pushing forward this movement. We are seeing how you are expanding this movement in Italy faster than we can keep up. Just like when you first went to India from the West, all of your young boy disciples were not able to keep up your pace as you so powerfully preached Kṛṣṇa consciousness throughout India. So we could see that you were not subject to the limitation of old age; therefore you never die but are still personally pushing forward this movement with even greater speed. We want to have the strength not to be left behind but to be of some use in your great mission.

You said that before coming to the West there were many translations of the *Bhagavad-gītā* but none of them were effective because not one of them gave the right conclusion, to surrender to Kṛṣṇa. Only since you have come to the West have so many fallen souls’ lives been converted to devotional service. This is the proof that you are the true *jagad-guru,* or the representative of the *paramparā.* So on this VYĀSA PŪJĀ day we are worshiping you as the true representative of Vyāsadeva. The actual representative of Lord Caitanya can be recognized by his ability to convert even doglike men into flowerlike devotees of the Lord. So certainly never before has there been such an *ācārya* who has traversed what, due to the influence of the Kali-yuga, has certainly become a hellish planet, and converted so many worse than doglike men into jubilant devotees and planted the seed of devotion in the hearts of millions through the vehicle of oceans of transcendental literatures.

Before you rescued me I had no hope in this life. Please let me not forget that your mercy is all that I am made of. When you were physically present, due to so many lusty desires within the heart I could not hear you nor appreciate who you actually are and the significance of this great movement. Somehow or other because all of my Godbrothers had taken shelter of you, they were turned into such great souls that they have been able to tolerate even me and continue to allow me to go on rendering what menial service I can to you.

I pray to never be separated from you, and you have said that this can be done by serving your instructions. So I am begging to always be allowed to serve you by serving in this movement of ISKCON. My mind is always being distracted by a myriad of fantasies of false enjoyment which, because they make me forget service to you, actually cause the greatest suffering.

Once you said that our hearts are like coal because even if you keep washing, it is only dirt. Even though my heart is so dark, I know that your potency is so great that if you allow me to somehow or other go on serving you by pushing on this movement you can turn coal into gold.

We in Italy, your disciples and granddisciples, are trying to push forward your mission by following your last order to cooperate together and not let personal interests impede the flood of love of Godhead. We realize, however, that the ability to do this is completely dependent on your mercy.

So on this most auspicious day we are all praying that you bestow your mercy on us by allowing us to go on working together to spread this great movement you have started.

Your mercy is all that we are made of.

## Johannesburg

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Our efforts to capture your magnificent spiritual qualities within the confines of these words can be compared to the attempts of a dwarf to catch the moon. Time, distance, and the sincerity of your earliest disciples seem to have brought into better perspective the multifaceted gems of *prema-bhakti* that you are giving to us so freely and earnestly.

How can we understand your love for Kṛṣṇa, your love for us, that took you from the heaven of Vṛndāvana to the hell of the Bowery, the same love that led you to walk miles in the midsummer Delhi heat and eventually collapse from heat exhaustion so that you could save a few *paise* from the bus ride and purchase paper for your *Back to Godhead* magazine?

The unending panorama of your great and noble heart, filled with the most wonderful personalities, Lord Gauranga and Lord Nityananda, Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa and all Their associates, is just beginning to unfold before our startled vision.

Thank you for coming to this rotten world, Śrīla Prabhupāda; your mercy is all that we are made of.

*Your humble servants,*

## Kathmandu

Dear Prabhupada,

Your Gurudeva had wanted that you preach the Bhagavata school  
To the English-speaking nations governed by tamasic rule,  
You heeded this instruction as your life’s goal,  
Preach, print books, beat the bṛhad-mṛdaṅga!  
Give the world that for which it hungers,  
Thus when the League of Devotees fell victim to a feud,  
To the West you came preaching “Spiritual sky is where your place is,”  
Armed with Bhagavatam you knew that “books were the basis.”

Dear Prabhupada,

Thus for one whole year you struggled seemingly alone  
So far away from Vṛndāvana, your eternal home,  
You came up against so many odds  
As you strived to preach to cats and dogs.  
But with unflinching faith in Kṛṣṇa, you carried on  
Undisturbed by the bleak skyscraper horizons,  
You took full pity  
Upon the masses of New York City  
And thus graced them with your divine presence  
Saying, “Preaching is the essence.”

Dear Prabhupada,

Gradually it was seen some men had come to you  
Chanting and dancing in Tompkins Square Park  
Your vibrant message soon filled their minds and hearts,  
You rented a storefront down on Second Avenue  
And advertised Gitā class to the curious few,  
Soon initiations followed and thus you felt assured  
You had nurtured a new movement, an incarnation of the Lord,  
“Nothing can check us,” you would say, “Kṛṣṇa is invincible!”  
“Just try to understand that utility is the principle.”

Dear Prabhupada,

Since that first U.S.A. appearance  
You circled the globe fourteen times,  
And established a worldwide ISKCON  
So perfect and sublime,  
Yet the world hardly knew that you came  
So humble you were, no desire for fame,  
And the world hardly knew when you were gone  
Except your dearmost disciples who form your ISKCON,  
Now your instructions remain to ever charter our course  
Lest we forget that “purity is the force.”

Dear Prabhupada,What you have done no one can undo  
And we pray that we may never become  
A burden unto you.  
“Books are the basis  
Preaching is the essence  
Utility is the principle  
Purity is the force.”

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

The glories of devotional service as described in your wonderful books are meant to be tasted by everyone. To give this sweet taste is your ardent desire because you are the most merciful deliverer of love of Kṛṣṇa. The darkness of this miserable age is so dense, but your blessing is so brilliant that even to the most foolish everything will become revealed by the force of your unfathomable love of Kṛṣṇa!

Devahuti calls her son, Lord Kapila, her “transcendental eye only attained after many, many births,” and we also have become fortunate after countless births by receiving your blessing of eternal life, full of bliss and knowledge. Now we are trying to give this gift we have received from you, the holy name of Kṛṣṇa. The more we give away your gift, the more you are giving.

As we try and give this gift, even here in the Hindu kingdom, preaching problems are the same. We are searching and working for our university degrees, our blue jeans and nice wife, but we cannot see the woods for the trees. The big problem of life (*janma, mṛtyu, jarā, vyādhi)* we will never stop to solve. With our youth, beauty, wealth, and position, still we are no better than the hogs, dogs, camels, and asses described in your *Śrīmad-Bhagavatam.* Your message is so heavy and sweet! It is difficult to resist, but it makes no compromise for our foolishness.

O divine spiritual master! If our service here to your ISKCON can only be a minute fraction of yours to Lord Caitanya then by your mercy so much can be accomplished. Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bless us all so that this preaching burden shall be the burden of love till everyone is dancing and chanting and remembering your name.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, please accept this clumsy attempt to glorify you in this VYĀSA PŪJĀ offering!

*Your insignificant eternal servitors,*

## Laguna Beach

*Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

O Vaisnava Thākura, you are my master, please let me be your dog. We can learn from the dog how to remain faithful to our master. A dog who has a good master does not have to think about his own maintenance; he simply acts as his master dictates, and he remains happy as a faithful dog. Whenever you call, day or night, I will jump to execute your order. Actually, that is your mercy, and it is a fortunate person who hears your order and jumps at the opportunity to satisfy your heart’s desire. Śrīla Prabhupāda, without your mercy there is no power even to execute the regulative practices of *bhakti,* so I must worship you at every moment of my life. O Śrī Gurudeva, you are the personification of the bliss of Kṛṣṇa’s mercy, and you are the storehouse of compassion for the poor and suffering conditioned souls for you have left behind so much transcendental knowledge, which is the only light for this dark world we live in. Your shining example of pure devotion and ecstatic love for Śrī Gauranāṅa is my inspiration to remain in this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement birth after birth serving the servants of the servants of your servant, one hundred times removed.

*Your eternal servants from New Jhārikhaṇḍa,*

## Lima

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your feet. Each day the extraordinary importance of your mission is more clear in our minds. It is unbelievable for us how you have expanded this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement and thus offered to all the world the opportunity of overcoming the *mleccha-dharma* platform and taking the single process of *sanātana-dharma,* as described in *Bhagavad-gītā.*

Who can be so foolish as to doubt your transcendental greatness and potency? Anybody with half a brain can understand that this change of *mleccha-dharma* to *sanātana-dharma* is historically, socially, and economically, what to speak of spirituality, the single alternative and the real solution to this so-called occidental civilization that is becoming more and more enmeshed in this ocean of ignorance in this age of Kali.

What could be more important and beneficial? When we meditate on these things our intelligence can’t find bounds to the greatness of your exalted position.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda! What we will never understand is how such fools and fallen souls like us could in some way attain your mercy.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, let us always remain in your service.

*Your fallen servants in Lima Mandira,*

## London

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

The wonder of our lives is here!  
The meaning of our lives is here!  
How blessed is this day that rises  
Illumining the sky of the heart with love  
Prabhupada has come! Prabhupada has come! Prabhupada has come!  
On this day we want to serve you,  
On this day let all your dreams come true,  
On this day, and every other  
Let us give our lives to you.  
Would that all the world were chanting, would that all the world were dancing,  
Would that we could help to rouse them from their deathly fallen plight.  
Let us be but rays of mercy at your loving glance,  
So now they’ll waken, regain their vision, and thus be saved at last.  
“Behold the two Lords, Nitāi and Gaura! How They dance and shine!”  
Your eyes of love behold Their glory,  
“So merciful They are” you try to say, ‘though your voice is choked  
Ecstatic tears flow from your heart—Vṛndāvana is uncloaked.  
“Let the holy name of Gauracandra spread across the land! In all the cities,  
towns and villages far flung across the earth,  
Let there be the song of life and love in each and every street  
Let karatālas chime, mṛdaṅga sweet, let a million voices sing  
Let the holy name, so brightly dancing, rouse each and every soul!”  
Come businessmen, and magnates with all your big schemes,  
Come women of fashion, come men with all your dreams,  
Come out of your office and your club—just come and chant and dance with us,  
Come young and old, black or white. Begin at last! Just make a start!  
Śrī Harināmā, You have so appeared, amidst a desolate waste  
Of broken hearts and broken dreams, Your kindness knows no bounds.  
Prabhupada has borne You on and on, so carefully in his haste,  
You and he have saved the fallen, so we can just resound—-  
Prabhupada has come! Prabhupada has come! Prabhupada has come!  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, please accept this worthless offering and our fervent hope,  
That on this day will all your dreams come true,  
For on this day and every other,  
We’re going to give our lives to you!

*Your servants,*

## Long Island

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. We, your children and grandchildren, have found existence in this world of illusion to be unbearable. Being bereft of the shelter at Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet is the most unfortunate position. However, your appearance in this world as *āśraya-vigraha* (the manifestation of the form of the Lord of whom one must take shelter) has engendered a most fortunate opportunity for all such suffering *jīvas* to return to the shelter of Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet. Therefore, we offer you our prostrated obeisances again and again. With childlike enthusiasm we jump and clap our hands, chanting “*jaya* Prabhupada” in appreciation of the mercy you’ve shown us. People in general view our unusual behavior and criticize us as being sentimentalists and fanatics, sometimes even mad. Being averse to your mercy, they cannot enter into the ecstasy of a loving relationship with you. They are most unfortunate.

After considering the extreme rarity of contacting a pure devotee in this material world, and the even greater rarity of accepting his instructions, we feel we have become the most fortunate souls in the entire universe. Despite our lack of qualifications, you have showered your kindness upon us by releasing us form shackles of material existence. In *māyā,* that can again bind us in the form of desire to enjoy your benedictions. In the *Śrī Caitanya-caritamrta( Antya* 20.52, purport) you say, “A pure devotee has no way of sensing happiness, except by seeing that Kṛṣṇa is happy. If Kṛṣṇa becomes happy in giving the devotee distress, then that unhappiness is the greatest of all happiness. Those who are materialistic regard their own happiness as the aim of life. Some aspire to enjoy by sharing the happiness of Kṛṣṇa. This is the mentality of a fruitive worker.” Śrīla Prabhupāda, kindly protect us from this insidious mentality. Help us to remain forever grateful to you, to sacrifice all selfish desires, and being imbued with the desire to please you, to sacrifice all selfish desires, and being imbued with the desire to please you, let us serve you cooperatively through the *saṅkīrtana-yajña.* Śrīmathi Rādhārani expresses this mood to please Lord Kṛṣṇa in this way:

*nā gaṇi āpana-duḥkha, sabe vāñchi tāṅra sukha,*  
*tāṅra sukha—-āmāra tātparya*  
*more yadi diyā duḥkha, tāṅra haila mahā-sukha,*  
*sei duḥkha—mora sukha-varya*

**“I do not mind My personal distress. I only wish for the happiness of Kṛṣṇa, for His happiness is the goal of My life. However, if He feels great happiness in giving Me distress, that distress is the best of My happiness.” (Cc.** ***Antya*** **20.52)**

Similarly, we pray for the desire to please you as the confidential servant of Kṛṣṇa. Our only desire is to give up all personal consideration, to serve your lotus feet, and to love you eternally.

*From the devotees at Long Island,*

## Los Angeles

**Śrīla Prabhupāda-jayāṣṭakam**

*virahākula-sakhya-daśā-gamita*  
*mita-loka-gateśvara-saukhya-vraja*  
*vraja-dhāmaga nitya-sumitra-jana*  
*janatā-pavana prabhupāda jaya*

*viraha-ākula-sakhya –*friendship in the mood of separation (*vipralambha-sakhya); dāśā –*condition; *gamita—*attained ; *mita-loka—-*to the material world; *gata—*gone; *īśvara—*of the Supreme Personality of Godhead; *saukhya –*of happiness; *vraja—*multitude; *vraja-dhāma-ga—*come from Goloka Vṛndāvana; *nitya—*eternal (ever); *su-mitra-jana—*well-wisher; *janatā—*of the people; *pavana—*purifier; *Prabhupada—*O Śrīla Prabhupāda; *jaya—-*all glories to you.

**O Śrīla Prabhupāda, all glories to you, our ever well-wisher and friend who left Kṛṣṇa’s abode in a mood of** ***vipralambha-sakhya*** **to increase Krnsa’s pleasure by reclaiming the fallen conditioned souls.**

*jaya rūpa-yatīśvara-tulya-guro*  
*guru-nātha-pareṣṭi-subuddhī-vara*  
*vara-bhakti-pracāraṇa-śaktī-bhara*  
*bhara-hāra-kara prabhupāda jaya*

*jaya—*all glories; *rūpa—*Rūpa Gosvāmī; *yati-īśvara –*the best of *sannyāsīs; tulya—*equal; *guro—*significance; *guru-nātha—*of your *guru; para—*transcendental; *iṣṭi—*intentions; *subuddhi—*understanding; *vara—*excellent; *bhakti—*devotional service; *pracāraṇa—*for preaching; *śakti-bhara—*great potency; *bhara—*the burden of material illusion; *hāra-kara—*removing.

**O Śrīla Prabhupāda, all glories to you. As Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī understood the intentions of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu in His verse** ***yaḥ kaumāra-haraḥ,*** **you understood the intentions of Śrīla Bhaktidisshānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, who being pleased bestowed upon you all necessary potencies for preaching the** ***bhakti*** **cult and removing the burden of the world.**

*pṛthu-mārkiṇaloka-narādhamaka-*  
*makarandaka-bhāgavata-prabhava*  
*prabhaViṣṇu-padāmburuha-smaraṇa*  
*maraṇābhaya he Prabhupada jaya*

*pṛthu—*broad; *mārkiṇaloka—*in America; *narādhamaka—*for the lowest of mankind; *makarandaka—*nectar; *bhāgavata—*of *Śrīmad-Bhagavatam; prabhava—*source; *prabhaViṣṇu—*of the all-powerful Supreme Personality of Godhead; *pada-amburuha—*the lotus feet; *smaraṇa—*remembering; *maraṇa-abhaya—*with no fear of death; *he—*O.

**Remembering the lotus feet of the all-powerful Personality of Godhead and accepting a nearly suicidal mission, you fearlessly boarded the ship** ***Jaladuta,*** **risking your life to bring “ India’s message of peace and good will,” Mahāprabhu’s teachings of** ***Śrīmad-Bhagavatam,*** **to the Jagāi-Mādhāi—like populace of Mārkiṇoloka (America).**

*vyavasāya-śubhātmika-buddhi-pada-*  
*pada sauhṛda-nanda-sutopakṛta*  
*kṛta-yācana gaurava-śāsti-vrata*  
*vrati-vṛnda-vara prabhupāda jaya*

*vyavasāya-śubha-ātmika-buddhi-pada—*Śrīla Viṣvanātha Cakravartī Thākura’s commentary on the *vyavasāyātmikā buddhiḥ* verse; *pada—*personification; *sauhṛda—*because of friendship; *nanda-suta—*by the son of Nanda; *upakṛta—*aided; *kṛta-yācana—*offered prayers; *gaurava—*of the *guru; śāsti—*the order; *vrata—*dedicated; *vrati-vṛnda—*of saintly persons; *vara—*the best.

**O Śrīla Prabhupāda, best of all saintly persons, all glories to you, who personify Viśvanātha Cakravartī Thākuraṣ explanation of** ***vyavasāyāt-mikā buddhiḥ*** **(Bg 2.41). Your dedication to the order of your spiritual master was so intense and your prayers so earnest that your friend Kṛṣṇa had to come down to work on your behalf.**

*supurāṇa-parārka-prakāśa-sudhā-*  
*sudhanāvali-bhāva-subhāṣya-kala*  
*kali-naṣṭa-dṛśi-dyuti-hetu-śubha*  
*śubha-tīrtha-vara prabhupāda jaya*

*su-purāṇa—*of *Śrīmad-Bhagavatam,* the best of the *Purāṇas; para—*transcendental; *arka—*sun; *prakāśa—*manifestation; *sudhā—*nectar; *sudhana-āvali—*treasury; *bhāva—*“emotional ecstasies”; *su-bhāṣya—*eloquent commentary; *kala—*with expertise; *kali—*by Kali-yuga; *naṣṭa—*destroyed; *dṛśi—*sight; *dyuti—*splendor; *hetu—*cause; *śubha—*auspicious; *śubha-tīrtha-vara—-*turning all places into places of pilgrimage.

**O Śrīla Prabhupāda, all glories to you. Your “emotional ecstasies” stored in the treasury of the nectarean “Bhaktivedanta purports” give light to “…persons who have lost their vision due to the dense darkness of ignorance in the age of Kali.” “Because you carry the Personality of Godhead within in your heart, you turn all places into places of pilgrimage.”**

*para-jīvana-jīva-pramity-abhaya*  
*bhaya dhṛṣta-suduṣkṛtinām ajita*  
*jita-navya-nirīśvara-vāda-rata*  
*rata-bhakta-kula prabhupāda jaya*

*para-jīvaja-jīva—*life comes from life; *pramiti—*conclusive proof; *abhaya—*fearless; *bhaya—*intimidation; *dhṛṣta—*of the puffed-up; *suduṣkṛtinām—*demons; *ajita—*unconquerable; *jita—*defeated; *navya—*modern; *nirīśvara-vāda-rata—*atheistic scientists; *rata—*delighted; *bhakti—-*of devotees; *kula—-*community.

**O Śrīla Prabhupāda, all glories to you. Delighting your devotees in intimidating the “puffed-up” demons, you boldly exposed the rascaldom of modern atheistic scientists, unchallengeably proving that “life comes from life: and not from matter.**

*mati-śāṅkara-śūnya-matāli-ghana-*  
*Ghana-khaṇḍana mohana-śakti-hara*  
*Hari-Kṛṣṇa-surūpa-vilāsa-sura*  
*Sura-veda-prada Prabhupada jaya*

*Mati—*in the mind; *śāṅkara-śūnya-mata-āli—*of the philosophies of impersonalism and voidism; *ghana—*dense; g*hana—*cloud; *khaṇḍana—*dispersing; *mohana-śakti—*the bewildering potency; *hara—*removing; *hari—*of Lord Hari; *Kṛṣṇa—*all-attractive; *su-rūpa—-*beautiful form; *vilāsa—*pastimes; *sura—*nectar; *sura—*the devotees; *veda-prada—*enlightening.

**O Śrīla Prabhupāda, all glories to you who removed the bewildering clouds of impersonalism and voidism which were hovering in our minds by revealing the nectar of the all-attractive form and pastimes of Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead.**

*para-Kṛṣṇa-parāmṛta-nāma-rasa-*  
*rasanā-vara-nāṭa-prakāśa-para*  
*aparādhi-kulāśru-taraṅga-kara*  
*karuṇākula he prabhupāda jaya*

*para—*transcendental; *Kṛṣṇa—*of Lord Kṛṣṇa; *para—*superexcellent; *amṛta—*nectar; *nāma—*name; *rasa—*mellow; *rasanā—*on the tongue; *vara—*excellent; *nāṭa—*dancing; *prakāśa—*manifestation; *para—*devoted; *aparādhi-kula—*of the offenders; *aśru—*with tears; *taraṅga—*like waves; *kara—*causing; *karuṇā—*with mercy; *ākula—*filled; *he—*O.

**O merciful Śrīla Prabhupāda, all glories to you. Upon anyone who sees you, the holy name of Kṛṣṇa immediately descends, dancing on their tongue. Even the offenders who mockingly chant are later to be found stunned with a choked-up voice, crying torrents of tears in pure love of Kṛṣṇa.**

*Your servants in New Dvāraka,*

## Madras

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to all the previous *ācāryas.* Although none of us are qualified to personally address you, yet, by your causeless mercy we are able to sit here as your disciples and granddisciples and compose a few lines in glorification of your wonderful appearance and activities.

You have surely changed the course of world history by your single-handed preaching work. The mission you have begun, in the form of an international society of preachers in the line of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, will without a doubt claim victory over the whole world in due course of time, not leaving a single soul unaffected and unbenefited by the mercy of Kṛṣṇa. This material world is meant for endless suffering, birth after birth, but you have miraculously turned it into Vaikuṇṭha, the spiritual world, full of knowledge and bliss. Exposing scientists, politicians, humanitarians, and cheap *svāmīs* alike, all as bluffers, simply blind men leading the blind, and by giving the real medicine for curing the material disease, you have shown yourself to be the greatest welfare worker of all time, a great full moon of benediction compared to the fireflylike “do-gooders” who know nothing more than saving the shirt and coat of the drowning living entity.

Your qualities of mercy, gentleness, compassion, surrender to Kṛṣṇa, spiritual strength, courage, and determination are all without limits. There is no possibility of finding an end to them. You are the embodiment of Kṛṣṇa’s mercy and Śrīmati Rādhārani’s especially compassionate love for all the parts and parcels of Her Supreme Lord. You have given us back our eternal relationship with our beloved. You have exposed the unreal and revealed the real. You are the abode of all knowledge, love, purity, and devotion to Kṛṣṇa. The world will never witness such a personality as you again, though you may have millions of empowered representatives. You are the symbol of success of the spiritual potency. Kṛṣṇa has singled you out above all His devotees. We are so fortunate to be members of your camp, serving you and helping your service to Lord Kṛṣṇa. Having brought us this far, please never leave us behind, but always remind us of our rightful duty and keep us immersed in the ocean of love of Godhead.

*Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda! *Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupada!

*Thousands and millions of daṇḍavats from your humble servants at Madras,*

Paradheya dāsa / Rāma-Kṛṣṇa dāsa / Narottama dāsa / Uddhava dāsa / Trilokanātha dāsa / Uttamaśloka dāsa / Madhusmita dāsa / Bhadracāru dāsa / Nanda-kumāra dāsa / Gītā-govinda dāsa / Bhakta Solai-Muthu

## Madrid

*Akṣṇoḥ phalaṁ tvādṛśa-darśanaṁ hi*  
*Tanoḥ phalaṁ tvādṛśa-gātra-saṅgaḥ*  
*Jihvā-phalaṁ tvādṛśa-kīrtanaṁ hi*  
*Sudurlabhā bhāgavatā hi loke*

A pure devotee of the Lord, to you our most blessed and beloved Gurudeva, for this day is the most auspicious day in the entire creation. Because of your blessed appearance, the entire world is now given a chance to be saved from the horrible onslaught of repeated birth and death, and now everyone can develop pure love for Kṛṣṇa.

Everything about you, Śrīla Prabhupāda is perfect and complete. Everything about your appearance is beautiful and elegant. Everything about your speech is supreme truth and is soothing to the heart aching due to forgetfulness of Kṛṣṇa. Everything about your glance is compassionate and merciful to the fallen conditioned souls. Everything you do is very thoughtful and perfect. Everything you suggest becomes truth and reality. Everything you touch becomes sacred and wonderful. Everything you say is the perfection of words and meaning. Everything you write is revealing truth and pure love of Kṛṣṇa. Everything you think is of saving the fallen souls and of Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet. Everything about your instructions is eternal and inspiring to take up. Everything about your teachings is Kṛṣṇa’s, and you make them easy to accept. Everything about your lectures is vibrant and ecstatic truth and magnetic to all conditioned souls. Everything about your smile is the understanding of bliss and the soul’s contentment. Everything about your walk is assurance and the right path back to Godhead. Everything about your lotus feet shows the shelter for the whole universe and more. Everything about you cannot be complete in words, for your qualities are infinite. So please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this auspicious day of your appearance we beg to remain your eternal and insignificant servants because you are everything we are living for.

All glories, all glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda because you are everything and more.

*Your eternal servants in Madrid Yātrā*

## Malaysia

O Most glorious and venerable Vaisnava, please accept our worthless obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet a million times and yet again.

Lord Kṛṣṇa appears to us to be far away. Lord Caitanya seems the same, but fortunately we are able to perceive both Lord Kṛṣṇa and Lord Caitanya through Your Divine Grace.

So we are making this prayer on this most blessed of days: please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, always appear in our restless minds and remind us to clutch your lotus feet for they are the perfect shelter.

How much more we seem to know your glories now after your disappearance. For one by one your great transcendental visions and plans are beginning to unfold by the hard work of your representatives. As the greatness of ISKCON grows, so your greatness is revealed.

But as the more your greatness is revealed, the more insignificant and useless and unqualified we feel to actually call ourselves your disciples. How did this great show of mercy take place? How did we become recipients of your mercy—-to us it is inconceivable! As the song goes, *śrī-guru karuṇā-sindhu, adhama-janāra bandhu:* O spiritual master, ocean of mercy, and friend of the fallen souls, you are the teacher of everyone and the life of the fallen souls, you are the teacher of everyone and the life of all people. *Hā hā prabhu koro doyā, deho more pada-chāyā:* Be merciful unto me and give me the shade of your lotus feet.

*Jagad-guru* Śrīla Prabhupāda *ki jaya!*

*From your fallen but fortunate, eternal servants,*

Rāmaśaraṇa dāsa adhikārī / Jānakī-devī dāsī

## Manipur

**“Some behave very well but do not preach the cult of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, whereas others preach but do not behave properly. You simultaneously perform both duties, in relation to the holy name by your personal behavior and by your preaching. Therefore you are the spiritual master of the entire world, for you are the most advanced devotee in the world.” (Cc.** ***Antya*** **4.102-3)**

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our prostrated obeisances at your lotus feet. These above statements by Śrīla Sanātana Gosvāmī about HaridāsaThākura apply most aptly to Your Divine Grace. By your personal example you have revealed to us the high standard of devotional service that we, your disciples and granddisciples, have to achieve.

Whilst the offerings of flowers and poetic eulogies in this most auspicious day are certainly in the line of Vaisnava etiquette, the real mode of worship for all of us is to engage in following your *vāṇī.* In the *śāstras* it is said that the spiritual master always remains with those disciples who strictly follow his orders.

In this holy land of Manipur (which circumstantially you were not able to personally bless with the dust of your lotus feet, although you desired very much to visit) every home is a temple, every garden an abode of *tulasī,* the common greeting is *hari bol* or Hare Kṛṣṇa, every little boy an expert *mṛdaṅga* player, and every day a festival (and also a feast!) day. The speaker of the legislative assembly wears a Vaisnava attire, complete with *gpoīcandana-tilaka,* even on public and official engagements.

But, alas! Kali has not exempted even this holy land from his grips, and inauspicious symptoms of materialism, viz: lust, greed and undue anger, have started appearing. Invaders have been introducing *mleccha* and *yavana* cultures, and the innocent members have fallen prey to this.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this auspicious occasion we pray that you kindly shower your blessings on us and give us the determination to serve Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa sincerely, whence Kali will flee of his own accord.

*Kṛṣṇa—sūrya-sama; māyā haya andhakāra,*  
*yāhān Kṛṣṇa tāhāṅ nāhi māyāra adhikāra*

Jaya Oṁ Viṣṇupada Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakācārya Aṣṭottara-śata Śrī Śrīmad A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Śrīla Prabhupāda *ki jaya.*

## Mauritius

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our eternal obeisances unto your lotus feet. All glories to you, dearmost sir, savior of the fallen, conditioned souls. All glories to Lord Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu, who so kindly sent you to deliver us.

Śrīla Narottama dāsaThākura sings:

*Yadi dayā na koribe, patita dekhiyā*  
*Patita-pavana nāma, kisera lagiya*

Lord Caitanya is known as *patita-pavana;* therefore, He must save us who are so fallen. So He has kindly sent you. *Toma vinā ke dayālu, jagat-saṁsāre.* There is no one else who is merciful in this miserable ocean of materialistic fools.

Lord Kṛṣṇa is Lord of everything and always fully satisfied; in relation to Him we are verily insignificant. However, you are expertly increasing the Lord’s pleasure and satisfaction, and so kindly using us for this purpose, by which even the Lord Himself does smile. What greater mercy is there than this? If possible, you are even more merciful than our dearest, beloved Lord.

Dear sir, kindly continue to engage us so. Please make us fit, just right to assist you in your loving service to the Lord.

Let us now take over the world, so you may offer it back to the Lord. Let us now take over this island, and make it New Śvetadvīpa Dhāma. Let the Vaisnavas thereby rejoice, which will surely please our Lord.

Please empower us. O lord, O master, dear well-wishing friend. Without you everything is impossible. Without Your Divine Grace these insignificant sparks will naturally die.

*Your servants and servants’ servants at ISKCON Mauritius,*

Aṇutama dāsa / Śrīniketana dāsa / Arjuna dāsa / Naṭavara dāsa / Apavarga dāsa / Gaura-kiśora dāsa / Govinda-pāda dāsa / Svayaṁrūpa dāsa / Nitāi Gaurāṅga dāsa / Daśaratha dāsa / Aśviniī Kumāra dāsa / Padmalocana dāsa / Devkīnandana-devī dāsī / Vaṁśī-vihārī-devī dāsī / Madana-mohana-mohinī-devī dāsī / Bhakta Dharma / Bhakta Das / Bhaktin Pratima / Bhaktin Amrita / Bhaktin Vijayanti / Bhaktin Raina / Bhakta Guruduth and family / Bhakta Boudnah / Bhakta Siva / Bhakta Ragubar / Bhaktin Ramdin / Bhakta Harischandra and family / Bhakta Marde / Bhakta Ajay / Bhakta Dhukit / Bhakta Atma and family / Bhakta Ashok and family and friends / and all the other thousands of life members, regular congregational members, well-wishers and friends.

## Mayapur

*To our most dear, eternal spiritual master, Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Gently woken by the sweet predawn chirping of Nadia’s “’citizens of the air,” King Lakhan Sen would rise and enter onto his jeweled palatial balcony. There, a cool and sacred breeze, carrying the fragrance of exotic flora and originating from the rapid swirling Ganges in the distant mist, would bathe the king in its opulence of pure goodness. Savoring a long, deep breath in those first dawning rays of the sun, King Sen would survey with great pleasure the vast exquisite beauty of his glorious Nadia, his Navadvīpa—capital city of this late-eleventh-century kingdom of Bengal.

Standing there, high up on Ballaldipi Hill, overlooking the whole of his magnificent Navadvīpa city—-standing there in the full sunlit glory of his reign—how could he have known, how could he ever have dreamed that within a few short years of this majestic vignette his entire beloved empire would be sacked and defeated by invading Muslim marauders? That the mass looting of the capital and royal palace would so weaken the kingdom that within a few short generations of this lightening conquest by Muhammadi Baktier-Khan in 1203 A.D., Navadvīpa and the whole of Bengal would come under the strong independent grip and rule of Muhammadan kings? How could he know that the then thriving and joyous Sen Dynasty was soon to be no more….?

Thus this magnificent little empire, despite it being situated in the most holy eternal *dhāmas,* went the way of all magnificent empires, big and small, which center all interest upon impure leaders who consider that they indeed, and not the Supreme Lord, King of kings, *should* be the center of all interest. The Greek, Roman, Mogul, English, and now the American empires, all fell or are falling—-all having been led by *rājas* who never had the sense to become *ṛṣis(* as per your brilliant but poorly-heeded instruction to P.M. Indira Gandhi some years back).

Therefore, it must be shown clearly to all who can see, and it must be broadcast loudly to all who can hear, that *yes, the first time* since Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa left this miserable planet over five thousand years ago, *your empire,* Śrīla Prabhupāda, is the first not to be made from that same stuff, in that same, nauseating, timeworn material mold. For you alone, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are the commissioned master architect, builder, and king of a most unique capital city and kingdom:

*Punaḥ kabhu prabhu icchā hoye balabān*  
*Habe māyāpure eirūp vāsasthān*

**“Later on, by dint of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s desire, a spiritual society will again be developed in Śrīdhar Mayapur.”**

*Eiṣab ghāṭ gaṅgātīre punar habe*  
*Prabhu mandira koriben bhakti sabe*

**“Again these** ***ghāṭas*** **will be built up on the banks of the Ganges, and the devotees will build the temples of the Lord.”**

*Adbhuta mandira eka haibe prakāśa*  
*Gauraṅgera nitya sebā haibe vikāsa*

**“And one wonderful temple will be manifest there from which the eternal worship of Lord Gaurāṅga will be spread.” - from the** ***Śrī Navadvīpa-Māhātmya (Parikrama Khaṇḍa,*** **Ch.4) by Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Thākura**

By the unfathomably profound devotion within your great heart, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to Their Supreme Lordships Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gauranga and Their desires (and to the desires of the great previous *ācāryas),* you have won the singular honor of becoming their dearest prediction-fulfiller. Just as you fulfilled the desires of your beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda by successfully bringing the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement to the West; just as you have fulfilled the desires of your beloved Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Thākura to see the fortunate Westerners dancing in the ecstasy of *hari-nāma-sankirtana* side by side with the Bengali devotees; and just as you are fulfilling the prophecy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu that the holy name of Śrī Kṛṣṇa will be heard and sung in every town and village, so again, before our very own astounded eyes, you are presently translating the above prophetic truths, spoken by the Supreme Lord Nityananda Prabhu to Śrīla Jiva Gosvami one day on *parikrama,* into concrete reality.

\[Be one spiritually inclined or inclined towards *pāpa* and *aparādha,* it pays to live in Śrīdhama Mayapur. You’ve heard about the Kazi. Now hear of Old King Sen. When Lord Nityananda used to conduct His thirteen-day, sixteen-*krośa parikramas* of Navadvīpa dhama, He would constantly use the ruins of the former king’s castle as a landmark to direct the devotees’ *sankirtna* movements. Thus the king received the eternal spiritual blessings of posthumous *ajñāta sukṛti….\]*

The creation of a borderless, spiritual empire whose center is the capital city of Śrīdhama Mayapur, whose source of preaching strength is that “one wonderful temple,” your Vedic Planetarium,” providing all of the transcendental inspiration required by your “spiritual society” of ISKCON to spread the message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness to all living entities without discrimination and without limit—-this is what you are unfolding before those fortunate few who can actually see and hear.

Thus in the broadest, eternal sense, it is your spiritual inheritance, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to be a constantly successful winner. And as it is fundamental to the *jīva* that we all want to go with such a winner, wherever he might be kind enough to take us, we pray to eternally remain with you within your ever-exciting, ever-unfolding preaching *līlā.* We beg to take birth again and again on the construction site of your truly glorious spiritual empire of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

*Your aspiring servants of Śrīdhāma Māyāpur,*

## Melbourne

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
I remember you preaching so strong and bold,  
Lord Kṛṣṇa’s pastimes all did unfold  
A glimpse of transcendence you have shown  
A guiding hand—the way back home.

In the night you came to dispel Kali’s dark,  
Light of the Bhāgavata—that was your mark;  
Glowworms appear and shimmer awhile,  
Victims, no doubt, of your radiant smile.

I remember you coming, striding so fast,  
Stand aside, Kali, the Vaishanva must pass,  
Souls to reclaim, a mission most urgent  
Lifetimes wasted—-all assets spent.

To the four corners you traveled to weed us all out  
Of the mire of nescience –we stood firm and stout;  
The expert gardener, the perfect tool  
You gave Kṛṣṇa-bhakti to even this fool.

By your mercy, dear Prabhupada, we have all come together,  
Taking shelter of you from Kali’s bad weather;  
Alone we are lost, united we must stay,  
For this, dear Prabhupada, your mercy we pray.

*From the devotees of Melbourne Mandira,*

## Mexico City

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Permit me to glorify you on this blessed day of your appearance. If Ananta Śeṣa with His many mouths is incapable of describing the unlimited transcendental qualities of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, what to speak of us, who are ordinary beings! How can we become qualified to describe with our limited mind and words your marvelous, divine qualities?

*yāṅra cite Kṛṣṇa-premā karaye udaya*  
*tāṅra vākya, kriyā, mudrā vijñeha na bujhaya*

**“Even the most learned man cannot understand the words, activities, and symptoms of a person situated in love of Godhead.” (Cc.** ***Madhya*** **23.29)**

In this age of Kali-yuga, you are the incarnation of mercy for all the living entities, for in this age, according to the *śāstras* (scriptures), the human being has absolutely no good qualities—-he has short life, he is quarrelsome, lazy, unfortunate, disoriented and always disturbed. It is only because of you that we are able to remain saved from this influence, by following the four regulative principles and chanting sixteen rounds daily.

Once a reporter said to you that if you were really a representative of God, perform some miracle. And you answered, “What greater miracle do you wish to see? Just see these girls and boys who before coming to Kṛṣṇa consciousness were addicted to sinful life—they were expert in violating the four principles: eating meat, taking intoxicants, gambling, and having illicit sex life. But now they are saints, dedicated twenty-four hours a day to glorifying God.”

It is said that the disciple can never repay this favor to the spiritual master because it is an invaluable favor, but he can show his gratitude by assisting in his divine mission of preaching God consciousness throughout his life.

Dear Gurudeva, we desire not only to express our profound gratitude to you through these words but also to dedicate ourselves to our fullest capacity in distributing your books, making more devotees, opening more temples, being more pure, and doing whatever else will expand this marvelous *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

*Your insignificant disciples from Mexico City Mandira*

## Miami

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Please accept my obeisances at your lotus feet.

*Devahūtir uvāca*  
*Nirviṇṇā nitarāṁ bhūmannn*  
*Asad-indriya-tarṣaṇāt*  
*Yena sambhāvyamānena*  
*Prapannāndhaṁ tahaḥ prabho*

**Devahuti said, “I am sick of the disturbance caused by my material senses, for because of this sense disturbance, my Lord, I have fallen into the abyss of ignorance.”**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have been servants of the material senses long enough in 8,400,000 species of life. We have realized that the senses make very poor masters; instead of giving us nectar, as we have desired, they have given us poison, which we have enthusiastically drunk, being overcome with lust, anger, and greed.

*Hari hari! biphale janama goṅāinu*  
*Manusya-janama pāiyā, Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa nā bhajiyā*  
*jāniyā śuniyā biṣa khāinu*

*golokera prema-dhana, hari nāma sankirtana,*  
*rati nā janmilo kene tāy*  
*saṁsara-biṣānale, dibā niśi hiyā jwale,*  
*juṛāite nā koinu upāy*

**“O Lord Hari, I have spent my life uselessly. Having obtained a human birth and having not worshiped Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, I have knowingly drunk poison. The treasure of divine love of Goloka Vṛndāvana has descended as the congregational chanting of Lord Hari’s holy names. Why did my attraction for that chanting never come about? Day and night my heart burns from the fire of the poison of worldliness, and I have not taken the means to relieve it.”**

The only way to overcome this lust is to gain your association, which is the most effective means of purifying the heart. It is, in fact, more purifying than repeated bathings of the Ganges or visiting all the holy places.

*Yat-pāda-saṁsrayāḥ sūta*  
*Munayaḥ praśamāyanāḥ*  
*Sadyaḥ punanty upaspṛṣṭāḥ*  
*Svardhuny-āpo ‘nusevayā*

**“O Suta, those great sages who have completely taken shelter of the lotus feet of the Lord can at once sanctify those who come in touch with them, whereas the waters of the Ganges can sanctify only after prolonged use.” (*****Bhāg.*** **1.1.15)**

The question is how to approach you and how to associate with you in the proper mood. The proper mood , of course, is the serving mood: to think always of giving pleasure or satisfying the master even at the expense of one’s own pleasure. This mood is taught to us by the *gopīs,* in that they are willing to go to hell to give Lord Kṛṣṇa pleasure. We should follow in their footsteps in approaching you. Our only pleasure should be your pleasure, and because you are so dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa that will be His pleasure. Because you are only desiring the pleasure of Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa is only desiring your pleasure.

*sādhavo hṛdayaṁ mahyaṁ*  
*sādhūnam hṛdayaṁ tv aham*  
*mad-anyat te na jānanti*  
*nāhaṁ tebhyo manāg api*

**“The pure devotee is always within the core of My heart, and I am always in the heart of the pure devotee. My devotees do not know anything but Me, and I do not know anyone else but them.” (*****Bhāg.*** **9.4.68)**

So, to develop this service attitude towards Your Divine Grace, we simply need to hear of your unlimited qualities and our minds will be naturally attracted to serving you. When one hears of a great person’s qualities his mind is drawn to that person like iron filings are to a magnet, and his service attitude develops spontaneously. Devahūti describes some of your good qualities in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (3.25.21).

*Titikṣavaḥ kāruṇikāḥ*  
*Suhṛdaḥ sarva-dehinām*  
*Ajāta-śatravaḥ śāntāḥ*  
*sādhhavaḥ sādhu-bhūṣaṇāḥ*

**“The symptoms of a** ***sādhu*** **are that he is tolerant, merciful, and friendly to all living entities. He has no enemies, he is peaceful, he abides by the scriptures, and all his characteristics are sublime.”**

*Tolerant:* Who can estimate what you have to tolerate from us. But just like a father teaching his child how to walk, watching the child stumble and fall, but remaining steady in his determination to train the child, you have patiently watched is in our awkward attempt to become Kṛṣṇa conscious, never removing your helping hand.

*Merciful:* Merciful means to give something wonderful that is not deserved by the recipient of the gift. Certainly we had no qualification to receive Kṛṣṇa. In fact, our qualification had earned us eternal separation from the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Still, amazingly, causelessly, you gave us your benediction. How can we repay you?

*Friendly to all living entities:* We have watched as you patiently without discrimination gave mercy to all living entities in all different types of bodies, advising us in each instance how to approach each conditioned soul. You were not simply interested in Indian living entities or human living entities, but in all creatures both moving and nonmoving.

*He has no enemies:* Even though many were, and are, opposed to this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, they too received your merciful glance as their ever well-wisher.

*Peaceful:* Your mind always remains fixed on Kṛṣṇa in spite of all our errors in judgment and your having to correct these errors.

*Follows scriptures:* All of your activities are directly from the pages of *Śrīmad-Bhagavatam.* You are in fact *Śrīmad-Bhagavatam* personified. The *Prabhupada-līlāmṛta,* written by your pure disciple Satsvarūpa Maharaja, should be considered as part of the *Śrīmad-Bhagavatam.*

These are some of your sublime characteristics, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and by constantly remembering these we can appreciate you in a personal way and our service attitude will expand unlimitedly.

*Your servants of the Miami Mandira,*

## Mombasa

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

On this occasion of your glorious appearance day, we are earnestly imploring you to make your appearance in the core of our hearts to stop the ravaging and plundering of the sinful desires that are threatening to overrun and totally destroy our feeble position on the battlefield of the senses.

We are begging you, with your formidable weapons of transcendental knowledge and love, to protect us from the mighty soldiers of lust, anger, and greed, which are scorching us with their fierceness.

Śrīla Prabhupada, after you’ve saved us from this hellish condition, please smash us with your weapons of chastisement (which are actually expressions of love) so that we will never again even think of deviating from your instructions even an inch.

To do so is indeed worse than drinking poison. There may be some medicinal remedy to counteract the effects of drinking ordinary poison. But no power on heaven or earth can save a disciple who deviates from the instructions of his spiritual master from the deadly poison of repeated birth and death.

Therefore, Śrīla Prabhupāda, our fervent prayer and request to you is that you kindly beat us very severely with your stick so that we will come to our senses and fully surrender to your lotus feet.

*In your service, Śrīla Prabhupāda, ISKCON Mombasa*

## Monterrey

*Ācāryas, GBC, sannyāsīs, and devotees in general:*

We are celebrating the eighty-fifth anniversary of the appearance of our beloved spiritual master, Oṁ Viṣṇupāda Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakācārya Aṣṭottara-śata Śrī Śrīmad A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Maharāja Śrīla Prabhupāda.

This VYĀSA PŪJĀ homage which we are celebrating today has a special flavor because we can submerge ourselves in the nectar that emanates from the pages of Śrīla Prabhupāda’s *Līlāmṛta.* We cab open the book, begin to turn the pages and situate ourselves in the First Chapter on the first of September of 1896, in a small house in a suburb of Calcutta. On this glorious day Śrīla Prabhupāda decided to appear to save us from the clutches of illusion. From his very advent, it could be seen that he was not an ordinary person, and his mission was predicted by an astrologer: “ When this child reached the age of seventy, he would cross the ocean, become a great exponent of religion, and open 108 temples.”

We can read page after page and saturate ourselves with spiritual strength that enables us to become closer than ever to His Divine Grace, to understand his transcendental activities to a certain degree, and to increase our loving service and attachment to his divine lotus feet; and thus we can pray as follows:

*śrī-guru-caraṇa-kamala bhaja mana*  
*guru-kṛpā vinā nahi koi sādhana-bala*  
*bhaja mana bhaja anukṣaṇa*

**“O mind, just worship the lotus feet of Śrī Guru. Without the mercy of the** ***guru*** **there is not power to execute the regulative practice of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.”**

This is also confirmed by the *Śrimad-Bhāgavatam* as follows: *yat-sevayā bhāgavata.* “By rendering service at the lotus feet of the spiritual master, one becomes capable of developing transcendental ecstasy in the service of the Personality of Godhead.”

*śrī-guru-caraṇa-padma kevala-bhakati-sadma*  
*bando mui sāvadhāna mate*  
*jāhāra prasāde bhāī, e bhava toriyā jāi*  
*Kṛṣṇa-prāptī hoy jāhā ha’ te*

**“The lotus feet of the spiritual master are the abode of pure devotional service. I bow down before those lotus feet with great respect and attention. My dear brother (my dear mind), it is by the grace of the spiritual master that we can cross the ocean of material existence and obtain Kṛṣṇa.”**

We should understand this very clearly—that the only means of freeing ourselves from the complications of material existence is by rendering service to our beloved spiritual master. This is confirmed by Ṛṣabhadeva in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* as follows: *mahat-sevāṁ dvāram āhur vimukteḥ.* “One can attain the path of liberation from material bondage only by rendering service to highly advanced spiritual personalities.”

A similar statement is found in the First Canto of the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam:*

*Yeṣāṁ saṁsmaraṇāt puṁsāṁ*  
*Sadyaḥ śuddhyanti vai gṛhāḥ*  
*Kiṁ punar darśana-sparśa-*  
*pāda-śaucāsanādhibhiḥ*

**“There is no doubt that one becomes freed of all reactions from sinful activities after visiting a devotee or touching his lotus feet or giving him a sitting place. Even by just remembering the activities of such a Vaisnava one becomes purified, then what to speak of rendering direct service to him?”**

The perfection of our lives will come about when we surrender ourselves completely to the divine lotus feet of Śrīla Prabhupāda.

*Guru-mukha-padma-vākya, cittete koriyā aikya,*  
*ār nā koriho mane āśā*

**“Make the teachings from the lotus mouth of the spiritual mastert one with your heart, and do not desire anything else.”**

To my beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda: we are all assembled here to offer you the greatest VYĀSA PŪJĀ homage that you have ever received. We have prepared ourselves by winning the December marathon of all the small temples in Latin America, by regularly reading your books, and by trying to act always for your satisfaction.

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, allow us to offer you within our heart a beautiful *vyāsāsana,* sturdily constructed with the understanding of your instructions, strongly supported by four feet made of religion (by following the four regulative principles), and decorated with all good qualities of a devotee (by our becoming your faithful servants), and allow us to decorate it with bluish-black velvet of thoughts of Kṛṣṇa, with a gold border forged of the *sankirtana* movement of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu. The base upon which it rests will be the powerful distribution of books. Daily and eternally we will clean it with the chanting of sixteen rounds.

All the devotees are returning now with a great garland of rescued souls, and they are placing it around your neck, which is making your fame spread more and more.

The *guru-pūjā* is beginning, and all the devotees offer at your lotus feet thousands of thousands souls rescued by the distribution of your books. The *bṛhad-mṛdaṅga* is spreading its sweet sounds to every darkened corner of this planet.

*Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Allow us to participate eternally in your *līlā.*

Vrajendra-kumāra dāsa brahmacari / Rāma-Govinda dāsa adhikārī / Jīvan-Kṛṣṇa dāsa brahmacari / Vidhūttama dāsa adhikārī / Gopāla dāsa adhikārī / Rāmasena dāsa brahmacari / Hare Kṛṣṇa dāsa brahmacari / Sadāśiva dāsa brahmacari / Rāma Svarūpa dāsa brahmacari / Caitanyadeva dāsa brahmacari / Kumuda dāsa brahmacari / Uttama-śloka dāsa brahmacari / Nara-Nārāyaṇa dāsa brahmacari / Bhakta Servando / Bhakta Eduardo / Bhakta Fernando / Girisutā-devī-dasi, Rādhā-Govinda dāsa / Rādhā-Ṭhākurāṇī-devī dāsī / Siddhārtha, Aniruddha / Satyā-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Beatriz / Bhaktin Regina

## Montreal

We offer countless obeisances at your lotus feet for you are the only life and soul. Since time immemorial, we are leading an aimless life, always baffled in our attempts to enjoy and control. Material nature has been both merciless and merciful in continually kicking us to make us ultimately sincerely desire relief, and thus came the fortune of attaining your lotus feet.

We are like beggars, and you are like a merciful, wealthy person who gives unreservedly. Having heard your ambrosial words we consider that there is nothing else to be had in life.

You are teaching us the very instruction of Lord Kṛṣṇa, who came to this world to reestablish the principles of religion. Thus you give causeless mercy to whoever becomes a follower in that direction. In return we are attempting to serve you in the way a poor man tries to pay back a debt although he is bankrupt.

Tears are flowing from our eyes at the realization of your causeless mercy and we remain agape. A feeling of shame overcomes us for not being able to express our gratitude properly. Kindly do not consider the mistakes and defects of this humble offering.

*Your servants,*

## Murwillumbah

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**I offer my most humble obeisances to my beloved** ***guru,*** **who is so dear to Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. The penances and austerities that Śrīla Prabhupāda underwent in his endeavor to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the Western world prove to us that he was the greatest preacher of the mission of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu.**

At the Vṛndāvana festival in 1974 Śrīla Prabhupāda desired that his disciples go to Bombay to perform *sankirtana* in the streets in order to counteract the demoniac forces that were causing trouble for our movement. Śrīla Prabhupāda was calling for his troops to do battle with the agents of *māyā.* By Prabhupada’s mercy I was able to go to Bombay with him and assist him. In the mornings, Śrīla Prabhupāda would go for his morning walk along Juhu Beach, preaching very strongly to eight or ten Indian gentlemen. After breakfast *prasadam* we would go out on *kīrtana,* chanting for hours around the Juhu neighborhood, Ville Parle, and Santa Cruz. Then in the evening His Divine Grace would give *Bhagavad-gītā* class under the little tin-roofed temple to the one to two hundred guests who would come each evening. After four days, the magic of Śrīla Prabhupāda’s preaching broke the demons’ attack and the trouble abated. How fortunate I felt at that time assisting you, in such an insignificant way, in your battle against the agents of Kali-yuga. How glorious it was to be with the supreme general directing his troops against the forces of *māyā.* Your personal courage and strength based on absolute faith in the Supreme Lord deeply convinced me of the process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Now, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have gone off to join the Supreme Lord in His abode. So my only request is that I pray for your mercy so that I can still remain as one of your loyal troops, to live amongst the members of your army, and follow the orders of those in the chain of command that you had established. The war against *māyā* is a very long, tough battle. But you have issued the orders and given the command. O most perfect general, please grant me the mercy that I may follow your instructions and make them my life and soul.

*Your humble servants from New Govardhana farm,*

## Nairobi

*Naivopayanty apacitiṁ kavayas taveśa*  
*Brahmāyuṣāpi kṛtam ṛddha-mudaḥ smarantaḥ*  
*Yo’ natar bahis tanu-bhṛtām aśubhaṁ vidhunvann*  
*ācārya-caitta-vapuṣā sva gatiṁ vyanakti*

**“O my Lord! Transcendental poets and experts in spiritual science could not fully express their indebtedness to you, even in a lifetime of Brahmā, for You appear in two features—externally as the** ***ācārya*** **and internally as the Supersoul—to deliver the embodied living being by directing him how to come to You.”**

As Uddhava expressed his inability to properly glorify the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Kṛṣṇa, so repeatedly at this time of year, we are acutely conscious of our inability to properly glorify our beloved founder-*ācārya,* spiritual master, and eternal preceptor, His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupada. Whatever the extent of homage, it can never be sufficient to repay his causeless mercy and compassion on us.

Despite our shortcomings in this regard, the offering of our humble service and homage on this auspicious occasion must be attempted by all faithful disciples. It is a direct result of such offerings that we become eligible to be favored with the capacity of assimilating transcendental knowledge.

We remember Śrīla Prabhupāda’s own magnificent VYĀSA PŪJĀ offering made in the year 1936 and reflect on the oceans of pleasure that surely rose in the heart of his spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvati Maharaja, on hearing the expert articulation of his disciple. One stanza gave particular pleasure to his Gurudeva:

Absolute is sentient, thou hast proved,  
Impersonal calamity, thou hast moved.

As Rūpa Gosvāmi had been able to compose a verse that expressed the inner thinking of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, so Śrīla Prabhupāda was able to know the mind of his spiritual master. In this regard *Śrīmad-Bhagavatam* states,

*Uttamaś cintitaṁ kuryāt*  
*Prokta-kārī tu madhyamaḥ*  
*Adhamo ‘śraddhayā kuryād*  
*Akartoccaritaṁ pituḥ*

**“A son who acts by anticipating what his father wants him to do is first class, one who acts upon receiving his father’s orders is second class, and one who executes his father’s orders is irreverently is third class. But a son who refuses his father’s orders is like stool.”**

Undoubtedly Śrīla Prabhupāda was qualified as a first-class son of his spiritual master. Even before accepting initiation, Śrīla Prabhupāda had drawn the attention of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta. He had observed his future disciple’s eagerness to hear and it gave him great pleasure.

In the concluding words of *Caitanya-caritāmṛta,* written in 1974, Śrīla Prabhupāda remarked that even though Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta is not physically present, he is confident that his spiritual master is very pleased by his work of translation.

Then for Śrīla Prabhupāda’s very last day in this world he had a disciple read him his spiritual master’s commentary on *Śrīmad-Bhagavatam.*

Factually, if we consider each and every moment of Śrīla Prabhupāda’s life we do not find one moment when he was not rendering some pleasing service to his spiritual master. From their first meeting in 1922 until Śrīla Prabhupāda’s departure from this world to return to Kṛṣṇa’s supreme abode, his only concern was to please his spiritual master.

It is inconceivable that we will ever be able to render such pleasing service as Śrīla Prabhupāda gave to his spiritual master. However, on this auspicious occasion we would request that sometime in our coming crores of births we might be allowed the opportunity of rendering some small fragment of pleasing service at the feet of our eternal master, His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupada.

*Your eternal servants at ISKCON Nairobi,*
